**File: `src/app/audio/setup.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/audio/setup.ts
import { Howl } from 'howler';
import { HowlerClipPlayer, type HowlCtor } from '../../engine/audio/howlerPlayer';
import { AudioManager } from '../../engine/audio/manager';
import { EMPTY_MANIFEST, ManifestClipSource, type ClipManifest } from '../../engine/audio/manifest';
import { createWebSpeechTts, pickVoice, type VoiceLike } from '../../engine/audio/tts';
import type { ContentPack } from '../../engine/content/load';

/**
 * The voice clips' manifest, from under the site's base path (a GitHub Pages project site lives under /<repo>/, where
 * '/audio/…' would 404 and every line fall back to TTS without an error). No manifest means TTS for every line.
 */
export async function loadManifest(baseUrl: string, fetchFn: typeof fetch = fetch): Promise<ClipManifest> {
  try {
    const res = await fetchFn(`${baseUrl}audio/manifest.json`);
    if (!res.ok) return EMPTY_MANIFEST;
    const m = (await res.json()) as Partial<ClipManifest> | null;
    return m?.version === 1 && m.clips && typeof m.clips === 'object' ? (m as ClipManifest) : EMPTY_MANIFEST;
  } catch {
    return EMPTY_MANIFEST;
  }
}

/** Spec §7's chain: clips under `${baseUrl}audio/lines` through Howler, then the device's speech. */
export function createAudioManager(pack: ContentPack, manifest: ClipManifest, baseUrl: string): AudioManager {
  return new AudioManager({
    pack,
    clips: new ManifestClipSource(manifest, `${baseUrl}audio/lines`),
    player: new HowlerClipPlayer(Howl as unknown as HowlCtor),
    tts: createWebSpeechTts(),
  });
}

/** An empty WAV: enough to build a Howl that never plays. */
const SILENCE = 'data:audio/wav;base64,UklGRiQAAABXQVZFZm10IBAAAAABAAEAQB8AAEAfAAABAAgAZGF0YQAAAAA=';

/**
 * Call at start-up. Howler registers its unlock listeners only when the first Howl is built, and that unlock fills its
 * html5 pool; a clip built before the child's first tap could otherwise fail and fall through to TTS.
 */
export function primeHowler(): void {
  new Howl({ src: [SILENCE], format: ['wav'], html5: true, preload: false });
}

/**
 * Call inside the child's first tap, before any await: iOS Safari ignores speak() outside a gesture until one has run
 * inside one (and fires no event to say so); Chrome reports 'not-allowed'.
 */
export function unlockSpeech(): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
  const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance('');
  u.volume = 0;
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(u);
}

/**
 * Chrome lists no voices until 'voiceschanged': ask at start-up. Logs the voice TTS will use on this device (macOS and
 * iOS list novelty voices; Chrome's network voices fail offline) — worth checking once per tablet.
 */
export function primeVoices(log: (message: string) => void = console.info): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) return;
  const report = () => {
    const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices() as unknown as VoiceLike[];
    if (voices.length > 0) log(`speech voice: ${pickVoice(voices, ['en-IN', 'en-GB', 'en-US', 'en'])?.name ?? 'none in English'}`);
  };
  report();
  window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener?.('voiceschanged', report, { once: true });
}

/** Stop speaking when the app is hidden (another app, a locked screen). Returns the unsubscribe. */
export function stopWhenHidden(stop: () => void, doc: Document = document): () => void {
  const onChange = () => {
    if (doc.visibilityState === 'hidden') stop();
  };
  doc.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onChange);
  return () => doc.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onChange);
}
```

### Task 12: Game rules: the record clock, the write queue, and feedback as data

Three small foundations. `monotonicClock` gives record times that never go back or repeat (mastery, bridging, the picker, `levelFor` and `replayRound` all order by `at`), starting after the last record. The write queue keeps the child moving while storage writes run one after another; a failed write is counted (§13: the parent corner, Plan 1C, shows a quiet warning) and never stops the next; anything that reads history waits for it to be idle. `stepFlow` is §4.4's feedback as a pure function: a first miss brings a hint, a second Buddy showing the answer; a wiggle after a quiet spell is a hint too; the round's outcome is `correct` only with no hint at all (Plan 1A: `corrected` does not count). Misses count per key — the round, or an item where each item has its own place (sort, match, sequence), so one slip each on two items gives two hints, not a demonstration.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/game/clock.ts`, `src/app/game/storage.ts`, `src/app/game/roundFlow.ts`
- Test: `src/app/game/game.test.ts`, `src/app/game/roundFlow.test.ts`

```ts
// src/app/game/game.test.ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { monotonicClock } from './clock';
import { createStorageHealth, guarded, WriteQueue } from './storage';

describe('monotonicClock', () => {
  it('follows the wall clock, but never goes back or repeats, and starts after the last record', () => {
    let wall = 1000;
    const clock = monotonicClock(5000, () => wall);
    expect(clock.now()).toBe(5001);
    wall = 9000;
    expect(clock.now()).toBe(9000);
    expect(clock.now()).toBe(9001);
    wall = 100; // the clock was set back
    expect(clock.now()).toBe(9002);
  });
});

describe('storage', () => {
  it('counts a failed write instead of throwing it', async () => {
    const health = createStorageHealth();
    vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {});
    expect(await guarded(health, async () => { throw new Error('quota'); })).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await guarded(health, async () => 7)).toBe(7);
    expect(health.failures).toBe(1);
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it('runs writes in order, goes on after a failure, and is idle once all are tried', async () => {
    const health = createStorageHealth();
    vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {});
    const done: number[] = [];
    const q = new WriteQueue(health);
    q.add(async () => { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5)); done.push(1); });
    q.add(async () => { throw new Error('quota'); });
    q.add(async () => { done.push(3); });
    await q.idle();
    expect(done).toEqual([1, 3]);
    expect(health.failures).toBe(1);
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });
});
```

```ts
// src/app/game/roundFlow.test.ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { FLOW_START, stepFlow, type FlowEvent, type FlowState } from './roundFlow';

const run = (...events: FlowEvent[]) => events.reduce<{ state: FlowState; effects: string[] }>((acc, e) => {
  const { state, effect } = stepFlow(acc.state, e);
  return { state, effects: [...acc.effects, effect] };
}, { state: FLOW_START, effects: [] });

const miss = (key: string, wrongId = key): FlowEvent => ({ type: 'miss', key, wrongId });
const done: FlowEvent = { type: 'done' };

describe('stepFlow (spec §4.4)', () => {
  it('right before any hint is correct', () => {
    expect(run(done).state).toMatchObject({ outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, wrongIds: [] });
  });

  it('a first miss brings a hint, and right after it is corrected', () => {
    const r = run(miss('round', 'b'), done);
    expect(r.effects).toEqual(['hint', 'end']);
    expect(r.state).toMatchObject({ outcome: 'corrected', hints: 1, wrongIds: ['b'] });
  });

  it('a second miss on the same key: Buddy shows the answer, and the round is shown', () => {
    const r = run(miss('round', 'b'), miss('round', 'p'), done);
    expect(r.effects).toEqual(['hint', 'show', 'end']);
    expect(r.state).toMatchObject({ outcome: 'shown', hints: 1, wrongIds: ['b', 'p'] });
  });

  it('counts misses per item: one miss each on two items is two hints, not a demonstration', () => {
    const r = run(miss('apple@l', 'small'), miss('pear@s', 'big'), done);
    expect(r.effects).toEqual(['hint', 'hint', 'end']);
    expect(r.state).toMatchObject({ outcome: 'corrected', hints: 2 });
  });

  it('a wiggle after a quiet spell is a hint: right after it is corrected', () => {
    const r = run({ type: 'wiggle' }, { type: 'wiggle' }, done);
    expect(r.effects).toEqual(['wiggle', 'wiggle', 'end']);
    expect(r.state).toMatchObject({ outcome: 'corrected', hints: 2 });
  });

  it('ignores everything once the round has ended', () => {
    const r = run(done, miss('round'), { type: 'wiggle' }, done);
    expect(r.effects).toEqual(['end', 'none', 'none', 'none']);
    expect(r.state).toMatchObject({ outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, wrongIds: [] });
  });

  it('never changes the state it is given', () => {
    const before = run(miss('round')).state;
    const copy = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(before));
    stepFlow(before, miss('round'));
    expect(before).toEqual(copy);
  });
});
```

**File: `src/app/game/clock.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/game/clock.ts
/**
 * Record times that never go back or repeat: mastery, bridging, the picker, levelFor and replayRound all order a
 * profile's records by `at`, and one step back (a clock change, two writes in one millisecond) reorders them.
 */
export interface Clock {
  now(): number;
}

/** Starts after `last` (lastRecordedAt) and moves on by at least 1 ms per reading. */
export function monotonicClock(last: number, wall: () => number = Date.now): Clock {
  let prev = last;
  return {
    now() {
      prev = Math.max(wall(), prev + 1);
      return prev;
    },
  };
}
```

**File: `src/app/game/storage.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/game/storage.ts
/** Spec §13: a failed storage write keeps the game going in memory; the parent corner (Plan 1C) shows a quiet warning. */
export interface StorageHealth {
  failures: number;
  lastError: unknown;
}

export const createStorageHealth = (): StorageHealth => ({ failures: 0, lastError: null });

/** Runs one write; a failure is counted and logged instead of thrown. */
export async function guarded<T>(health: StorageHealth, write: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | undefined> {
  try {
    return await write();
  } catch (e) {
    health.failures += 1;
    health.lastError = e;
    console.error('storage write failed', e);
    return undefined;
  }
}

/**
 * Writes run one after another, without holding the child up. Anything that reads history — the next set, Buddy's
 * suggestion — waits for idle() first, so it sees every round already played.
 */
export class WriteQueue {
  private tail: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();

  constructor(private readonly health: StorageHealth) {}

  add(write: () => Promise<unknown>): void {
    this.tail = this.tail.then(async () => {
      await guarded(this.health, write);
    });
  }

  idle(): Promise<void> {
    return this.tail;
  }
}
```

**File: `src/app/game/roundFlow.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/game/roundFlow.ts
import type { Outcome } from '../../engine/progress/types';

/**
 * Spec §4.4's feedback rules for one round, as data. Misses are counted per key: 'round' where the round has one answer
 * or one set of answers (tap, tap-all, give, scenes), the item where each item has its own place (sort, match,
 * sequence). The first miss on a key brings a hint; the second, Buddy showing the answer.
 */
export interface FlowState {
  readonly misses: Readonly<Record<string, number>>;
  /** hints shown: one per first miss, one per wiggle after a quiet spell (a repeated prompt is not a hint) */
  readonly hints: number;
  /** a hint has pointed at an answer, so what follows is `corrected` at best */
  readonly hinted: boolean;
  /** Buddy has shown an answer, so the round is `shown` */
  readonly shown: boolean;
  /** what the child picked wrongly, in order */
  readonly wrongIds: readonly string[];
  /** set when the round ends */
  readonly outcome: Outcome | null;
}

export const FLOW_START: FlowState = Object.freeze({ misses: Object.freeze({}), hints: 0, hinted: false, shown: false, wrongIds: Object.freeze([]), outcome: null });

export type FlowEvent =
  | { type: 'miss'; key: string; wrongId: string }
  | { type: 'wiggle' }
  | { type: 'done' };

/** What an event calls for: a hint, a demonstration, a quiet-spell wiggle, the round's end — or nothing, once it has ended. */
export type FlowEffect = 'hint' | 'show' | 'wiggle' | 'end' | 'none';

export function stepFlow(state: FlowState, event: FlowEvent): { state: FlowState; effect: FlowEffect } {
  if (state.outcome !== null) return { state, effect: 'none' };
  switch (event.type) {
    case 'miss': {
      const n = (state.misses[event.key] ?? 0) + 1;
      const next = { ...state, misses: { ...state.misses, [event.key]: n }, wrongIds: [...state.wrongIds, event.wrongId] };
      return n === 1
        ? { state: { ...next, hints: state.hints + 1, hinted: true }, effect: 'hint' }
        : { state: { ...next, shown: true }, effect: 'show' };
    }
    case 'wiggle':
      return { state: { ...state, hints: state.hints + 1, hinted: true }, effect: 'wiggle' };
    case 'done':
      return { state: { ...state, outcome: state.shown ? 'shown' : state.hinted ? 'corrected' : 'correct' }, effect: 'end' };
  }
}
```

### Task 13: Game rules: preparing, recording and finishing a set; stickers

The set runner uses the engine as Plan 1A's hand-off says. `prepareSet` waits for every earlier write, then: the picker chooses the template (rotation, bridging); `levelFor` takes the level from every one of its set results; the goal's attempts give the history; a fresh random seed. After 10 minutes a six-round set plays its first five — a prefix, so it still replays (§4.4). `recordRound` queues the attempt under the session its history was built for; `finishSet` records the set's result only when its last round has ended (a set left early keeps its attempts and has none). A failed read gives the empty history: the child plays on. Stickers (§4.6) take a picture from the set's answers — a letter is drawn as a letter — about one in fifteen is shiny, and each goes on its island's page.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/game/setRunner.ts`, `src/app/game/stickers.ts`
- Test: `src/app/game/setRunner.test.ts`

```ts
// src/app/game/setRunner.test.ts
import 'fake-indexeddb/auto';
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { readPack } from '../../engine/content/readPackFromDisk';
import { generateSet } from '../../engine/generator/round';
import { attemptsForGoal, createDb, createProfile, startSession, type BuddyDB } from '../../engine/progress/db';
import { replayRound } from '../../engine/progress/history';
import { monotonicClock } from './clock';
import { finishSet, prepareSet, recordRound, roundsToPlay, suggestNext, type PlayContext, type RoundResult } from './setRunner';
import { newSticker, pageOf, stickerPicture } from './stickers';
import { createStorageHealth, STORAGE_WAIT_MS, WriteQueue } from './storage';

const pack = readPack();
let db: BuddyDB;
let n = 0;
let ctx: PlayContext;

beforeEach(async () => {
  db = createDb(`runner-${++n}`);
  const profile = await createProfile(db, { name: 'Asha', buddy: { colour: 'blue', name: 'Gajju', accessory: 'cap' } });
  const session = await startSession(db, profile.id);
  const storage = createStorageHealth();
  let seed = 100;
  ctx = {
    pack, db, profileId: profile.id, sessionIndex: session.index, sessionStartedAt: 0, clock: monotonicClock(0), now: () => 60_000,
    writes: new WriteQueue(storage), storage, random: () => 0.5, newSeed: () => ++seed,
  };
});

const right: RoundResult = { outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, durationMs: 1000, wrongIds: [] };

describe('roundsToPlay', () => {
  it('plays the whole set, and five rounds once the session has passed 10 minutes', () => {
    expect(roundsToPlay(6, 9 * 60_000)).toBe(6);
    expect(roundsToPlay(6, 10 * 60_000 + 1)).toBe(5);
    expect(roundsToPlay(4, 30 * 60_000)).toBe(4);
  });
});

describe('a set, start to finish', () => {
  it('generates from the picked template at its level, and records rounds that replay', async () => {
    const set = await prepareSet(ctx, 'big-small');
    expect(set.template.topicId).toBe('big-small');
    expect(set.level).toBe(1);
    expect(set.rounds).toEqual(generateSet(pack, set.template, { seed: set.seed, level: 1, history: { recentItemIds: [], lastSlot: null, lastSceneId: null, lastVariantByLine: {} } }));
    for (const round of set.rounds) recordRound(ctx, round, { ...right, wrongIds: ['x'] });
    finishSet(ctx, set, set.rounds.map(() => right));
    await ctx.writes.idle();
    const attempts = await attemptsForGoal(db, ctx.profileId, set.template.goalId);
    expect(attempts.map((a) => replayRound(pack, attempts, a))).toEqual([...set.rounds]);
    expect(attempts[0]!.details?.wrongIds).toEqual(['x']);
  });

  it('rotates the game when the topic is played again, and raises each game\'s level after two good sets of it', async () => {
    const played: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const set = await prepareSet(ctx, 'big-small');
      played.push(`${set.template.id}@${set.level}`);
      for (const round of set.rounds) recordRound(ctx, round, right);
      finishSet(ctx, set, set.rounds.map(() => right));
    }
    expect(played).toEqual(['big-small.tap@1', 'big-small.sort@1', 'big-small.tap@1', 'big-small.sort@1', 'big-small.tap@2', 'big-small.sort@2']);
  });

  it('cuts a set to five rounds after 10 minutes, and the shorter set still replays', async () => {
    ctx.now = () => 11 * 60_000;
    const set = await prepareSet(ctx, 'letters');
    expect(set.rounds).toHaveLength(5);
    for (const round of set.rounds) recordRound(ctx, round, right);
    await ctx.writes.idle();
    const attempts = await attemptsForGoal(db, ctx.profileId, set.template.goalId);
    expect(attempts.map((a) => replayRound(pack, attempts, a))).toEqual([...set.rounds]);
  });

  it('suggests a topic on another island than the one just played', async () => {
    const set = await prepareSet(ctx, 'letters');
    recordRound(ctx, set.rounds[0]!, right);
    const next = await suggestNext(ctx);
    expect(pack.topics.get(next)!.subject).not.toBe('literacy');
  });

  it('plays on from empty histories when storage cannot be read', async () => {
    ctx.db = { ...db, attempts: undefined } as unknown as BuddyDB;
    const errors = console.error;
    console.error = () => {};
    try {
      const set = await prepareSet(ctx, 'feelings');
      expect(set.rounds.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(ctx.storage.failures).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    } finally {
      console.error = errors;
    }
  });
});

describe('a stalled storage', () => {
  it('never holds the next set up for good: a write or read with no answer is given up after STORAGE_WAIT_MS', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout'] });
    vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {});
    try {
      const stalled = { where: () => ({ equals: () => ({ toArray: () => new Promise(() => {}) }) }) };
      ctx.writes.add(() => new Promise(() => {}));
      ctx.db = { ...db, attempts: stalled, setResults: stalled } as unknown as BuddyDB;
      let set: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof prepareSet>> | undefined;
      void prepareSet(ctx, 'feelings').then((s) => { set = s; });
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(4 * STORAGE_WAIT_MS); // the write, then the picker's, the levels' and the goal's reads
      expect(set?.rounds.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(ctx.storage.failures).toBe(4);
    } finally {
      vi.useRealTimers();
      vi.restoreAllMocks();
    }
  });
});

describe('stickers', () => {
  it('take a picture from the set\'s answers', () => {
    const rounds = generateSet(pack, pack.templates.get('healthy-food.plate')!, { seed: 4, level: 1, history: { recentItemIds: [], lastSlot: null, lastSceneId: null, lastVariantByLine: {} } });
    const healthy = new Set([...pack.objects.values()].filter((o) => o.tags.healthy).map((o) => o.asset));
    for (const r of [0, 0.3, 0.99]) expect(healthy.has(stickerPicture(rounds, () => r))).toBe(true);
    const letters = generateSet(pack, pack.templates.get('letters.tap')!, { seed: 4, level: 1, history: { recentItemIds: [], lastSlot: null, lastSceneId: null, lastVariantByLine: {} } });
    expect(stickerPicture(letters, () => 0)).toMatch(/^glyph:/);
  });

  it('are shiny about one time in fifteen, and go on their island\'s page', () => {
    const rounds = generateSet(pack, pack.templates.get('feelings.tap')!, { seed: 4, level: 1, history: { recentItemIds: [], lastSlot: null, lastSceneId: null, lastVariantByLine: {} } });
    expect(newSticker('p', 1, 'feelings.tap', rounds, () => 0.05).shiny).toBe(true);
    expect(newSticker('p', 1, 'feelings.tap', rounds, () => 0.07).shiny).toBe(false);
    expect(pageOf(pack, { source: 'feelings.tap' })).toBe('wellness');
    expect(pageOf(pack, { source: 'brush-teeth' })).toBe('home');
  });
});
```

**File: `src/app/game/setRunner.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/game/setRunner.ts
import type { ContentPack } from '../../engine/content/load';
import type { Template } from '../../engine/content/schema';
import { generateSet } from '../../engine/generator/round';
import { EMPTY_HISTORY, type Level, type Round } from '../../engine/generator/types';
import { recordAttempt, recordSetResult, setResultsForTemplate, type BuddyDB } from '../../engine/progress/db';
import { levelFor } from '../../engine/progress/difficulty';
import { attemptFromRound, buildGeneratorHistory, buildPickerHistory } from '../../engine/progress/history';
import { EMPTY_PICKER_HISTORY, pickTemplate, suggestTopic, type PickerHistory } from '../../engine/progress/picker';
import type { Outcome } from '../../engine/progress/types';
import type { Clock } from './clock';
import { within, type StorageHealth, type WriteQueue } from './storage';

/** One visit's player and record keeping: who plays, in which session, and where the record goes. */
export interface PlayContext {
  pack: ContentPack;
  db: BuddyDB;
  profileId: string;
  sessionIndex: number;
  sessionStartedAt: number;
  /** record times (`at`) */
  clock: Clock;
  /** the wall clock, for the session's length */
  now: () => number;
  writes: WriteQueue;
  storage: StorageHealth;
  random: () => number;
  /** a fresh random 32-bit seed per set */
  newSeed: () => number;
}

export interface PlaySet {
  template: Template;
  level: Level;
  seed: number;
  /** the rounds to play: the whole generated set, or its first five once the session has passed 10 minutes */
  rounds: readonly Round[];
  /** the form the child just missed, when the set bridges back to a familiar one (Buddy says so before the first prompt) */
  bridgeFrom: string | null;
}

export interface RoundResult {
  outcome: Outcome;
  hints: number;
  durationMs: number;
  wrongIds: readonly string[];
}

/** Spec §4.4: a set is 6 rounds by default, 5 once the session has passed 10 minutes — a prefix of the set, so it replays. */
export function roundsToPlay(generated: number, sessionMs: number): number {
  return sessionMs > 10 * 60_000 && generated > 5 ? 5 : generated;
}

/**
 * A failed read, or one with no answer within STORAGE_WAIT_MS, gives the empty history: the child plays on, with less
 * variation, and the failure is counted.
 */
async function readOr<T>(ctx: PlayContext, read: () => Promise<T>, empty: T): Promise<T> {
  try {
    return await within(read());
  } catch (e) {
    ctx.storage.failures += 1;
    ctx.storage.lastError = e;
    console.error('storage read failed', e);
    return empty;
  }
}

async function pickerHistory(ctx: PlayContext): Promise<PickerHistory> {
  await ctx.writes.idle();
  return readOr(ctx, () => buildPickerHistory(ctx.db, ctx.profileId, ctx.sessionIndex), { ...EMPTY_PICKER_HISTORY, sessionIndex: ctx.sessionIndex });
}

/**
 * The next set for a topic (spec §5.4–5.5): the picker chooses the template (mechanic rotation, bridging), levelFor its
 * level from every set result, and the goal's attempts its history — read after every earlier write has landed.
 */
export async function prepareSet(ctx: PlayContext, topicId: string): Promise<PlaySet> {
  const { template, bridgeFrom } = pickTemplate(ctx.pack, topicId, await pickerHistory(ctx), ctx.random);
  const level = levelFor(await readOr(ctx, () => setResultsForTemplate(ctx.db, ctx.profileId, template.id), []));
  const history = await readOr(ctx, () => buildGeneratorHistory(ctx.db, ctx.profileId, template.goalId, ctx.sessionIndex), EMPTY_HISTORY);
  const seed = ctx.newSeed();
  const rounds = generateSet(ctx.pack, template, { seed, level, history });
  return { template, level, seed, bridgeFrom, rounds: rounds.slice(0, roundsToPlay(rounds.length, ctx.now() - ctx.sessionStartedAt)) };
}

/** Queue the attempt for a round the child just finished, under the session its set's history was built for. */
export function recordRound(ctx: PlayContext, round: Round, result: RoundResult): void {
  const attempt = attemptFromRound(round, { profileId: ctx.profileId, sessionIndex: ctx.sessionIndex, at: ctx.clock.now(), ...result });
  ctx.writes.add(() => recordAttempt(ctx.db, attempt));
}

/**
 * A finished set's result (spec §5.4 rule 7): only when its last round has ended — a set left early keeps its attempts
 * and stickers but has none. `correct` counts the rounds right before any hint.
 */
export function finishSet(ctx: PlayContext, set: PlaySet, results: readonly RoundResult[]): void {
  const result = {
    profileId: ctx.profileId, at: ctx.clock.now(), sessionIndex: ctx.sessionIndex, templateId: set.template.id, goalId: set.template.goalId,
    level: set.level, correct: results.filter((r) => r.outcome === 'correct').length, total: results.length,
  };
  ctx.writes.add(() => recordSetResult(ctx.db, result));
}

/** Buddy's suggested next spot (spec §5.4 rule 6), from a history that includes everything just played. */
export async function suggestNext(ctx: PlayContext): Promise<string> {
  return suggestTopic(ctx.pack, await pickerHistory(ctx), ctx.random);
}
```

**File: `src/app/game/stickers.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/game/stickers.ts
import type { ContentPack } from '../../engine/content/load';
import type { SubjectId } from '../../engine/content/schema';
import type { Payload, Round, RoundItem } from '../../engine/generator/types';
import type { Sticker } from '../../engine/progress/types';

/** Spec §4.6: rare shiny stickers, about one in fifteen. */
export const SHINY_CHANCE = 1 / 15;

/** The pictures a set's answers showed: what the child matched, sorted, gave or chose. */
function answerItems(p: Payload): readonly RoundItem[] {
  switch (p.kind) {
    case 'choose': return p.options.filter((o) => p.correctIds.includes(o.id));
    case 'pairs': return p.left;
    case 'bins': return p.items;
    case 'give': return p.items.filter((i) => p.correctIds.includes(i.id));
    case 'order': return p.items;
    case 'scenario': return p.choices.filter((c) => c.correct);
  }
}

/** Spec §4.6: each sticker is a topic-themed picture from the set just played (a letter is drawn as a letter). */
export function stickerPicture(rounds: readonly Round[], random: () => number): string {
  const pictures = [...new Set(rounds.flatMap((r) => answerItems(r.payload)).map((i) => i.asset))];
  return pictures[Math.floor(random() * pictures.length)] ?? 'ui/shiny.svg';
}

export function newSticker(profileId: string, at: number, templateId: string, rounds: readonly Round[], random: () => number): Omit<Sticker, 'id'> {
  return { profileId, at, pictureId: stickerPicture(rounds, random), source: templateId, shiny: random() < SHINY_CHANCE };
}

/** The sticker book's page for a sticker (spec §4.6: one page per island, plus Home for routine stickers, Plan 1C). */
export function pageOf(pack: ContentPack, sticker: Pick<Sticker, 'source'>): SubjectId | 'home' {
  const topicId = pack.templates.get(sticker.source)?.topicId;
  return (topicId && pack.topics.get(topicId)?.subject) || 'home';
}
```

### Task 14: A round's feedback: the useRoundFlow hook

The hook every mechanic plays its round through, on `stepFlow`'s rules. A miss: a soft sound, "Hmm, try again" (or the mechanic's own line: a kindness scene's reaction) and a brief hint on what the mechanic says it points at; a second miss on the same key: "It's this one!" and Buddy pointing, after which the mechanic shows the answer and goes on. A finished answer: the chime and a random cheer, unless Buddy showed it. A quiet spell: the prompt again after 8 s, then every 15 s with a wiggle that counts as a hint (§4.4) — timed from the child's last touch. Timers stop when the round ends or goes away (the Home button).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/game/useRoundFlow.ts`
- Test: `src/app/game/useRoundFlow.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**File: `src/app/game/useRoundFlow.test.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/game/useRoundFlow.test.ts
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { cueClass, QUIET, useRoundFlow, type FlowEnd, type Reaction } from './useRoundFlow';

function setup() {
  const said: string[] = [];
  const sounds: string[] = [];
  const reactions: Reaction[] = [];
  const ends: FlowEnd[] = [];
  let replays = 0;
  const deps = {
    speaker: { say: (id: string) => { said.push(id); return { caption: id, done: Promise.resolve() }; }, replayPrompt: async () => { replays += 1; } },
    sfx: { chime: () => sounds.push('chime'), boop: () => sounds.push('boop'), pop: () => sounds.push('pop') },
  };
  const hook = renderHook(() => useRoundFlow({
    deps,
    targets: (key) => (key === null ? ['answer'] : [`hint-for-${key}`]),
    onEnd: (e) => ends.push(e),
    onReact: (r) => reactions.push(r),
  }));
  return { hook, said, sounds, reactions, ends, replays: () => replays };
}

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

describe('useRoundFlow', () => {
  it('right first time: a chime, a cheer, and the round ends correct', () => {
    const t = setup();
    act(() => t.hook.result.current.done());
    expect(t.sounds).toEqual(['chime']);
    expect(t.said).toEqual(['fb.correct']);
    expect(t.reactions).toEqual(['cheer']);
    expect(t.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, wrongIds: [] }]);
    expect(t.hook.result.current.ended).toBe(true);
  });

  it('a first miss: a soft sound, "try again" and a hint on the answer; then right is corrected', () => {
    const t = setup();
    let next = '';
    act(() => { next = t.hook.result.current.miss('round', 'b'); });
    expect(next).toBe('retry');
    expect(t.sounds).toEqual(['boop']);
    expect(t.said).toEqual(['fb.try_again']);
    expect(t.hook.result.current.cue).toEqual({ ids: ['hint-for-round'], kind: 'hint' });
    expect(cueClass(t.hook.result.current.cue, 'hint-for-round')).toBe('cue-hint');
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(2000));
    expect(t.hook.result.current.cue).toBeNull(); // a hint is brief
    act(() => t.hook.result.current.done());
    expect(t.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'corrected', hints: 1, wrongIds: ['b'] }]);
  });

  it('a second miss on the same key: "it\'s this one", Buddy points, and the round is shown without a cheer', () => {
    const t = setup();
    act(() => { t.hook.result.current.miss('round', 'b'); });
    let next = '';
    act(() => { next = t.hook.result.current.miss('round', 'd', 'react.unkind'); });
    expect(next).toBe('show');
    expect(t.said).toEqual(['fb.try_again', 'fb.shown']);
    expect(t.hook.result.current.cue).toEqual({ ids: ['hint-for-round'], kind: 'show' });
    act(() => t.hook.result.current.done());
    expect(t.sounds).toEqual(['boop']);
    expect(t.reactions).toEqual(['hmm', 'point']);
    expect(t.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'shown', hints: 1, wrongIds: ['b', 'd'] }]);
  });

  it('says a mechanic\'s own line for a hint and for the cheer (a kindness scene\'s reactions)', () => {
    const t = setup();
    act(() => { t.hook.result.current.miss('round', 'laugh', 'react.unkind'); });
    act(() => t.hook.result.current.done('react.kind'));
    expect(t.said).toEqual(['react.unkind', 'react.kind']);
  });

  it('after a quiet 8 s repeats the prompt; every 15 s after, repeats it with a wiggle, which is a hint', () => {
    const t = setup();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUIET.first));
    expect(t.replays()).toBe(1);
    expect(t.hook.result.current.cue).toBeNull(); // the first repeat is not a hint
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUIET.every));
    expect(t.replays()).toBe(2);
    expect(t.hook.result.current.cue).toEqual({ ids: ['answer'], kind: 'hint' });
    expect(t.reactions).toEqual(['point']);
    act(() => t.hook.result.current.done());
    expect(t.ends[0]).toMatchObject({ outcome: 'corrected', hints: 1 });
  });

  it('a touch starts the quiet spell again, and nothing repeats after the round ends', () => {
    const t = setup();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUIET.first - 1000));
    act(() => t.hook.result.current.touch());
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUIET.first - 1000));
    expect(t.replays()).toBe(0);
    act(() => t.hook.result.current.step());
    expect(t.sounds).toEqual(['pop']);
    act(() => t.hook.result.current.done());
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000));
    expect(t.replays()).toBe(0);
  });

  it('ends once: a second done, a late miss or a step change nothing', () => {
    const t = setup();
    act(() => t.hook.result.current.done());
    act(() => { t.hook.result.current.done(); t.hook.result.current.miss('round', 'x'); t.hook.result.current.step(); });
    expect(t.ends).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(t.sounds).toEqual(['chime']);
  });

  it('stops its timers when the round goes away (the Home button)', () => {
    const t = setup();
    t.hook.unmount();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000));
    expect(t.replays()).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the hook**

**File: `src/app/game/useRoundFlow.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/game/useRoundFlow.ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { Outcome } from '../../engine/progress/types';
import type { Sfx } from '../audio/sfx';
import type { Speaker } from '../audio/speaker';
import { UI_LINES } from '../audio/uiLines';
import { FLOW_START, stepFlow, type FlowEvent, type FlowState } from './roundFlow';

/** Spec §4.4: after 8 s without input the prompt is repeated, then every 15 s with a hint (the answer wiggles). */
export const QUIET = { first: 8000, every: 15000 };
/** How long a hint or a demonstration stays on what it points at. */
export const CUE_MS = 1800;
/** How long a mechanic lets Buddy point at an answer before placing it or moving on. */
export const SHOW_MS = 1600;

/** What to wiggle (a hint) or point out (Buddy showing the answer). */
export interface Cue {
  readonly ids: readonly string[];
  readonly kind: 'hint' | 'show';
}

export type Reaction = 'cheer' | 'hmm' | 'point';

export interface FlowDeps {
  speaker: Pick<Speaker, 'say' | 'replayPrompt'>;
  sfx: Pick<Sfx, 'chime' | 'boop' | 'pop'>;
}

export interface FlowEnd {
  outcome: Outcome;
  hints: number;
  wrongIds: readonly string[];
}

export interface RoundFlow {
  readonly cue: Cue | null;
  readonly ended: boolean;
  /**
   * A wrong move. 'retry' after the first miss on `key` — a hint shows, and `line` (default "Hmm, try again") is said;
   * 'show' after the second — Buddy points at the answer: put it in place (or end the round) after SHOW_MS.
   */
  miss(key: string, wrongId: string, line?: string): 'retry' | 'show';
  /** A right move that is not the last (an item placed). */
  step(): void;
  /** The answer is complete: the round ends, with a cheer (`line`, default a random fb.correct) unless Buddy showed it. */
  done(line?: string): void;
  /** Any touch: the quiet-spell timer starts again. */
  touch(): void;
}

export interface RoundFlowOptions {
  deps: FlowDeps;
  /** what a hint for `key` points at; for null, what the quiet-spell wiggle points at */
  targets: (key: string | null) => readonly string[];
  onEnd: (end: FlowEnd) => void;
  onReact?: (reaction: Reaction) => void;
  quiet?: { first: number; every: number };
}

/** One round's feedback (spec §4.4), on the stepFlow rules: hints, demonstrations, cheers and the quiet-spell repeats. */
export function useRoundFlow(options: RoundFlowOptions): RoundFlow {
  const opts = useRef(options);
  opts.current = options;
  const state = useRef<FlowState>(FLOW_START);
  const [cue, setCue] = useState<Cue | null>(null);
  const [ended, setEnded] = useState(false);
  const quietTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined>(undefined);
  const cueTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined>(undefined);
  const repeats = useRef(0);

  const dispatch = (event: FlowEvent) => {
    const r = stepFlow(state.current, event);
    state.current = r.state;
    return r.effect;
  };

  const point = useCallback((ids: readonly string[], kind: Cue['kind']) => {
    clearTimeout(cueTimer.current);
    setCue({ ids, kind });
    cueTimer.current = setTimeout(() => setCue(null), CUE_MS);
  }, []);

  const arm = useCallback(() => {
    clearTimeout(quietTimer.current);
    const quiet = opts.current.quiet ?? QUIET;
    quietTimer.current = setTimeout(() => {
      if (state.current.outcome !== null) return;
      const o = opts.current;
      void o.deps.speaker.replayPrompt();
      if (repeats.current > 0 && dispatch({ type: 'wiggle' }) === 'wiggle') {
        point(o.targets(null), 'hint');
        o.onReact?.('point');
      }
      repeats.current += 1;
      arm();
    }, repeats.current === 0 ? quiet.first : quiet.every);
  }, [point]);

  const touch = useCallback(() => {
    if (state.current.outcome !== null) return;
    repeats.current = 0;
    arm();
  }, [arm]);

  useEffect(() => {
    arm();
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(quietTimer.current);
      clearTimeout(cueTimer.current);
    };
  }, [arm]);

  const miss = useCallback((key: string, wrongId: string, line?: string): 'retry' | 'show' => {
    const effect = dispatch({ type: 'miss', key, wrongId });
    const o = opts.current;
    if (effect === 'hint') {
      touch();
      o.deps.sfx.boop();
      o.deps.speaker.say(line ?? UI_LINES.tryAgain);
      point(o.targets(key), 'hint');
      o.onReact?.('hmm');
      return 'retry';
    }
    if (effect === 'show') {
      touch();
      o.deps.speaker.say(UI_LINES.shown);
      point(o.targets(key), 'show');
      o.onReact?.('point');
      return 'show';
    }
    return 'retry';
  }, [point, touch]);

  const step = useCallback(() => {
    if (state.current.outcome !== null) return;
    opts.current.deps.sfx.pop();
    touch();
  }, [touch]);

  const done = useCallback((line?: string) => {
    if (dispatch({ type: 'done' }) !== 'end') return;
    clearTimeout(quietTimer.current);
    const { outcome, hints, wrongIds } = state.current;
    const o = opts.current;
    if (outcome !== 'shown') {
      o.deps.sfx.chime();
      o.deps.speaker.say(line ?? UI_LINES.correct);
      o.onReact?.('cheer');
    }
    setEnded(true);
    o.onEnd({ outcome: outcome as Outcome, hints, wrongIds });
  }, []);

  return { cue, ended, miss, step, done, touch };
}

/** Whether a cue points at this id, and how: for a className. */
export function cueClass(cue: Cue | null, id: string): string {
  return cue && cue.ids.includes(id) ? `cue-${cue.kind}` : '';
}
```

### Task 15: Mechanics: tap-the-one and tap-all-that-match

The first two mechanics (spec §6 #1–2) and what every mechanic shares: its props (the frozen round, a speaker and effects, the names, Buddy's look, and where to report the end and Buddy's reactions) and the stage styles. Tap-the-one serves letters, CVC pictures, feelings and big/small (the same object at several sizes, on equal cards); a wrong tap shakes, the second shows the answer. Tap-all ticks each right one and is done when all are; a wrong tap is a miss for the round, and the second has Buddy tick the rest. Both judge by `correctIds`, never by slot (Plan 1A's notes). The test fakes give a seeded round of any template and record what a mechanic asked for.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/mechanics/types.ts`, `src/app/mechanics/mechanics.css`, `src/app/mechanics/TapTheOne.tsx`, `src/app/mechanics/TapAll.tsx`
- Create: `src/app/testing/fakes.ts`
- Test: `src/app/mechanics/tap.test.tsx`

```ts
// src/app/testing/fakes.ts
// Test doubles for the app: a speaker and sound effects that record what they were asked to do, and seeded rounds.
import { generateSet } from '../../engine/generator/round';
import { EMPTY_HISTORY, type Level, type Round } from '../../engine/generator/types';
import type { FlowEnd, Reaction } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import type { MechanicProps } from '../mechanics/types';
import { pack } from '../pack';

export { pack };

/** Round `index` of a template's set made with no history. */
export function roundOf(templateId: string, seed = 1, level: Level = 1, index = 0): Round {
  const template = pack.templates.get(templateId);
  if (!template) throw new Error(`no template ${templateId}`);
  const round = generateSet(pack, template, { seed, level, history: EMPTY_HISTORY })[index];
  if (!round) throw new Error(`no round ${index}`);
  return round;
}

export interface Recorder {
  said: string[];
  words: string[];
  sounds: string[];
  reactions: Reaction[];
  ends: FlowEnd[];
  replays: number;
}

/** Props for a mechanic under test, and what it did. */
export function mechanicProps(round: Round): { props: MechanicProps; rec: Recorder } {
  const rec: Recorder = { said: [], words: [], sounds: [], reactions: [], ends: [], replays: 0 };
  const props: MechanicProps = {
    round,
    deps: {
      speaker: {
        say: (id) => { rec.said.push(id); return { caption: id, done: Promise.resolve() }; },
        replayPrompt: async () => { rec.replays += 1; },
        word: async (id) => { rec.words.push(id); },
      },
      sfx: { chime: () => rec.sounds.push('chime'), boop: () => rec.sounds.push('boop'), pop: () => rec.sounds.push('pop') },
    },
    names: { name: 'Asha', buddy: 'Gajju' },
    look: { colour: 'blue', accessory: 'cap' },
    onEnd: (e) => rec.ends.push(e),
    onReact: (r) => rec.reactions.push(r),
  };
  return { props, rec };
}
```

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/tap.test.tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { mechanicProps, roundOf } from '../testing/fakes';
import { SHOW_MS } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { TapAll } from './TapAll';
import { TapTheOne } from './TapTheOne';

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

/** The option buttons, in screen order, with the round's own ids. */
function options(round: ReturnType<typeof roundOf>) {
  if (round.payload.kind !== 'choose') throw new Error('expected choose');
  const buttons = screen.getAllByRole('button');
  return round.payload.options.map((o, i) => ({ id: o.id, right: round.payload.kind === 'choose' && round.payload.correctIds.includes(o.id), button: buttons[i]! }));
}

describe('TapTheOne', () => {
  it('draws every option, and the right tap ends the round correct', () => {
    const round = roundOf('letters.tap', 3, 2);
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    render(<TapTheOne {...props} />);
    const opts = options(round);
    expect(opts).toHaveLength(3);
    fireEvent.click(opts.find((o) => o.right)!.button);
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, wrongIds: [] }]);
    expect(rec.sounds).toEqual(['chime']);
  });

  it('a wrong tap: try again and a hint; a second: Buddy shows the answer and the round ends shown', () => {
    const round = roundOf('big-small.tap', 5, 3);
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    render(<TapTheOne {...props} />);
    const opts = options(round);
    const wrong = opts.filter((o) => !o.right);
    fireEvent.click(wrong[0]!.button);
    expect(rec.said).toEqual(['fb.try_again']);
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([]);
    expect(opts.find((o) => o.right)!.button.className).toContain('cue-hint');
    fireEvent.click(wrong[1]!.button);
    expect(rec.said).toEqual(['fb.try_again', 'fb.shown']);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(SHOW_MS));
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'shown', hints: 1, wrongIds: [wrong[0]!.id, wrong[1]!.id] }]);
  });

  it('ignores taps once the round has ended', () => {
    const round = roundOf('feelings.tap', 2, 1);
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    render(<TapTheOne {...props} />);
    const opts = options(round);
    fireEvent.click(opts.find((o) => o.right)!.button);
    fireEvent.click(opts.find((o) => !o.right)!.button);
    expect(rec.ends).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rec.said).toEqual(['fb.correct']);
  });

  it('shows the same object bigger and smaller for a size round', () => {
    const round = roundOf('big-small.tap', 5, 3);
    render(<TapTheOne {...mechanicProps(round).props} />);
    const widths = screen.getAllByRole('img').map((img) => Number((img as HTMLImageElement).style.width.replace('px', '')));
    expect(new Set(widths).size).toBe(4); // four sizes of one object
  });
});

describe('TapAll', () => {
  it('ticks each right one; the round ends correct when all are ticked', () => {
    const round = roundOf('good-habits.clean', 4, 2);
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    render(<TapAll {...props} />);
    const right = options(round).filter((o) => o.right);
    expect(right).toHaveLength(3);
    right.forEach((o) => fireEvent.click(o.button));
    fireEvent.click(right[0]!.button); // a second tap on a ticked one changes nothing
    expect(rec.sounds).toEqual(['pop', 'pop', 'chime']);
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, wrongIds: [] }]);
  });

  it('after two wrong taps Buddy ticks the rest and the round is shown', () => {
    const round = roundOf('good-habits.clean', 4, 2);
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    render(<TapAll {...props} />);
    const opts = options(round);
    fireEvent.click(opts.find((o) => o.right)!.button);
    const wrong = opts.filter((o) => !o.right);
    fireEvent.click(wrong[0]!.button);
    fireEvent.click(wrong[1]!.button);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(SHOW_MS + 3 * 450));
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'shown', hints: 1, wrongIds: [wrong[0]!.id, wrong[1]!.id] }]);
    expect(opts.filter((o) => o.right).every((o) => o.button.getAttribute('aria-pressed') === 'true')).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
// src/app/mechanics/types.ts
import type { Round } from '../../engine/generator/types';
import type { Names, Speaker } from '../audio/speaker';
import type { Accessory, BuddyColour } from '../buddy/Buddy';
import type { FlowDeps, FlowEnd, Reaction } from '../game/useRoundFlow';

export interface BuddyLook {
  colour: BuddyColour;
  accessory: Accessory;
}

export interface MechanicDeps extends FlowDeps {
  speaker: Pick<Speaker, 'say' | 'replayPrompt' | 'word'>;
}

/** What every mechanic gets: the round (frozen — copy before reordering), what it needs to speak, and where to report. */
export interface MechanicProps {
  round: Round;
  deps: MechanicDeps;
  names: Names;
  look: BuddyLook;
  /** the round ended: its outcome, hints and wrong picks */
  onEnd: (end: FlowEnd) => void;
  /** Buddy in the corner reacts: a cheer, a "hmm", pointing */
  onReact: (reaction: Reaction) => void;
  quiet?: { first: number; every: number };
}
```

```css
/* src/app/mechanics/mechanics.css — shared by every mechanic */
.stage { position: relative; flex: 1; min-height: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: space-evenly; gap: 2vh; padding: 8px 20px 16px; }
.row { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: center; align-items: center; gap: clamp(14px, 3vw, 40px); }
.option {
  position: relative; display: grid; place-items: center; padding: 12px; min-width: var(--target); min-height: var(--target);
  background: var(--card) var(--grain); border: 3px solid var(--line); border-radius: var(--radius); box-shadow: var(--shadow);
  transition: transform 0.15s, box-shadow 0.2s, border-color 0.2s;
}
.option:active { transform: scale(0.96); }
.option.is-right { border-color: var(--good); box-shadow: 0 0 0 7px rgba(92, 158, 49, 0.35), var(--shadow); }
.option.is-faded { opacity: 0.45; }
.option__mark { position: absolute; right: -14px; top: -14px; width: 48px; height: 48px; pointer-events: none; }
.option__label { font-size: 17px; color: var(--ink-soft); margin-top: 4px; text-align: center; max-width: 180px; }
.stimulus { padding: 14px 22px; }

.draggable { touch-action: none; transition: transform 0.28s cubic-bezier(.3, 1.4, .5, 1); cursor: grab; }
.draggable.is-dragging { transition: none; z-index: 20; pointer-events: none; cursor: grabbing; filter: drop-shadow(0 14px 14px rgba(61, 47, 39, 0.25)); }
.draggable.is-selected { outline: 6px solid var(--glow); outline-offset: 3px; }

.tray { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: center; align-items: center; gap: 14px; min-height: 110px; }
.drop {
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 10px 16px; min-width: 150px;
  border: 4px dashed var(--line); border-radius: 30px; background: rgba(255, 250, 240, 0.7); transition: box-shadow 0.2s;
}
.drop__inside { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: center; gap: 4px; min-height: 52px; max-width: 260px; }
.drop__label { font-size: 17px; color: var(--ink-soft); }
.done-button { min-width: 96px; min-height: 96px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--card); border: 4px solid var(--good); box-shadow: var(--shadow); display: grid; place-items: center; }
.done-button img { width: 64px; height: 64px; }
.badge { position: absolute; left: -10px; top: -10px; width: 34px; height: 34px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--glow); font-weight: 700; display: grid; place-items: center; font-size: 20px; }
```

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/TapTheOne.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { cueClass, SHOW_MS, useRoundFlow } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { useLater, useViewport } from '../ui/hooks';
import { ItemView } from '../ui/ItemView';
import { tiltFor } from '../ui/tilt';
import type { MechanicProps } from './types';
import './mechanics.css';

/**
 * Spec §6 #1: 2–4 options, one right (letters, CVC pictures, the biggest of one object, feelings). Judged by
 * correctIds, never by slot. A wrong tap shakes and gives a hint; the second, Buddy shows the answer.
 */
export function TapTheOne({ round, deps, onEnd, onReact, quiet }: MechanicProps) {
  const p = round.payload;
  if (p.kind !== 'choose') throw new Error(`tap-the-one cannot play a ${p.kind} round`);
  const later = useLater();
  const { w, h } = useViewport();
  const [right, setRight] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState<{ id: string; n: number } | null>(null);
  const flow = useRoundFlow({ deps, targets: () => p.correctIds, onEnd, onReact, quiet });
  const size = Math.round(Math.min((w - 120) / p.options.length - 70, h * (p.stimulus ? 0.28 : 0.4), 250));

  const tap = (id: string) => {
    if (flow.ended || right !== null) return;
    flow.touch();
    if (p.correctIds.includes(id)) {
      setRight(id);
      flow.done();
      return;
    }
    setShake((s) => ({ id, n: (s?.n ?? 0) + 1 }));
    if (flow.miss('round', id) === 'show') {
      later(() => {
        setRight(p.correctIds[0] ?? null);
        flow.done();
      }, SHOW_MS);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="stage">
      {p.stimulus && <div className="card stimulus"><ItemView item={p.stimulus} size={Math.round(h * 0.26)} /></div>}
      <div className="row">
        {p.options.map((o) => (
          <button
            key={shake?.id === o.id ? `${o.id}:${shake.n}` : o.id}
            type="button"
            className={`option ${cueClass(flow.cue, o.id)} ${right === o.id ? 'is-right' : ''} ${shake?.id === o.id ? 'shake' : ''}`}
            style={{ width: size + 28, height: size + 28, rotate: tiltFor(o.id) }}
            aria-label={o.label}
            onClick={() => tap(o.id)}
          >
            <ItemView item={o} size={size} />
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/TapAll.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { cueClass, SHOW_MS, useRoundFlow } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { artUrl } from '../ui/art';
import { useLater, useViewport } from '../ui/hooks';
import { ItemView } from '../ui/ItemView';
import { tiltFor } from '../ui/tilt';
import type { MechanicProps } from './types';
import './mechanics.css';

/**
 * Spec §6 #2: 4–8 options, several right; done when every right one is tapped. A wrong tap shakes and counts as a
 * miss (a hint: the ones left wiggle); a second, and Buddy ticks the rest.
 */
export function TapAll({ round, deps, onEnd, onReact, quiet }: MechanicProps) {
  const p = round.payload;
  if (p.kind !== 'choose') throw new Error(`tap-all-that-match cannot play a ${p.kind} round`);
  const later = useLater();
  const { w, h } = useViewport();
  const [ticked, setTicked] = useState<readonly string[]>([]);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState<{ id: string; n: number } | null>(null);
  const left = p.correctIds.filter((id) => !ticked.includes(id));
  const flow = useRoundFlow({ deps, targets: () => left, onEnd, onReact, quiet });
  const cols = Math.ceil(p.options.length / 2);
  const size = Math.round(Math.min((w - 120) / cols - 60, (h - 260) / 2 - 40, 200));

  const tap = (id: string) => {
    if (flow.ended || ticked.includes(id)) return;
    flow.touch();
    if (p.correctIds.includes(id)) {
      const next = [...ticked, id];
      setTicked(next);
      if (next.length === p.correctIds.length) flow.done();
      else flow.step();
      return;
    }
    setShake((s) => ({ id, n: (s?.n ?? 0) + 1 }));
    if (flow.miss('round', id) === 'show') {
      left.forEach((rest, i) => later(() => setTicked((t) => [...t, rest]), SHOW_MS + i * 450));
      later(() => flow.done(), SHOW_MS + left.length * 450);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="stage">
      <div className="row" style={{ maxWidth: cols * (size + 90) }}>
        {p.options.map((o) => (
          <button
            key={shake?.id === o.id ? `${o.id}:${shake.n}` : o.id}
            type="button"
            className={`option ${cueClass(flow.cue, o.id)} ${ticked.includes(o.id) ? 'is-right' : ''} ${shake?.id === o.id ? 'shake' : ''}`}
            style={{ width: size + 24, height: size + 24, rotate: tiltFor(o.id) }}
            aria-label={o.label}
            aria-pressed={ticked.includes(o.id)}
            onClick={() => tap(o.id)}
          >
            <ItemView item={o} size={size} />
            {ticked.includes(o.id) && <img className="option__mark" src={artUrl('ui/done.svg')} alt="" />}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 16: Mechanics: drag, or tap then tap

Spec §11: tap and drag only. `useDnd` lets the child drag an item to a target — it follows the finger, glides back if let go anywhere else, and the target under the finger is found with `elementsFromPoint` (the dragged item ignores the pointer meanwhile) — or tap the item, then the target. A tiny wobble is a tap, and the click that ends a drag is not one. Mechanics decide whether a drop was right. jsdom has no layout, so the drag path is tested with a stubbed `elementsFromPoint`, and the drag mechanics' own tests play the tap way, through `testing/dom.ts`.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/mechanics/dnd.ts`, `src/app/testing/dom.ts`
- Test: `src/app/mechanics/dnd.test.tsx`

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { dropTargetAt, useDnd } from './dnd';

function Board({ onDrop }: { onDrop: (item: string, target: string) => void }) {
  const dnd = useDnd({ onDrop });
  return (
    <div>
      <button type="button" {...dnd.itemProps('apple')}>apple</button>
      <div {...dnd.targetProps('basket')}>basket</div>
    </div>
  );
}

afterEach(() => {
  delete (document as { elementsFromPoint?: unknown }).elementsFromPoint;
});

describe('useDnd', () => {
  it('a drag follows the finger and drops on the target under it', () => {
    const onDrop = vi.fn();
    render(<Board onDrop={onDrop} />);
    const item = screen.getByText('apple');
    document.elementsFromPoint = () => [screen.getByText('basket')];
    fireEvent.pointerDown(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 10, clientY: 10 });
    fireEvent.pointerMove(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 60, clientY: 90 });
    expect(item.style.transform).toContain('translate(50px, 80px)');
    expect(item.className).toContain('is-dragging');
    fireEvent.pointerUp(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 60, clientY: 90 });
    expect(onDrop).toHaveBeenCalledWith('apple', 'basket');
    expect(item.style.transform).toBe(''); // glides back to its place; the mechanic moves it if the drop was right
    fireEvent.click(item); // the click that ends a drag is not a tap
    expect(item.className).not.toContain('is-selected');
  });

  it('a tiny wobble is a tap, not a drag; let go over nothing and nothing is dropped', () => {
    const onDrop = vi.fn();
    render(<Board onDrop={onDrop} />);
    const item = screen.getByText('apple');
    document.elementsFromPoint = () => [];
    fireEvent.pointerDown(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 10, clientY: 10 });
    fireEvent.pointerMove(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 14, clientY: 12 });
    expect(item.className).not.toContain('is-dragging');
    fireEvent.pointerMove(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 200, clientY: 10 });
    fireEvent.pointerUp(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 200, clientY: 10 });
    expect(onDrop).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('tap the item, then the target (a second tap on the item puts it down)', () => {
    const onDrop = vi.fn();
    render(<Board onDrop={onDrop} />);
    const item = screen.getByText('apple');
    fireEvent.click(item);
    expect(item.className).toContain('is-selected');
    fireEvent.click(item);
    expect(item.className).not.toContain('is-selected');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('basket'));
    expect(onDrop).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    fireEvent.click(item);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('basket'));
    expect(onDrop).toHaveBeenCalledWith('apple', 'basket');
  });

  it('finds the nearest marked target under a point', () => {
    render(<div data-drop-id="bin"><span>inside</span></div>);
    document.elementsFromPoint = () => [screen.getByText('inside')];
    expect(dropTargetAt(5, 5)).toBe('bin');
  });
});
```

**File: `src/app/mechanics/dnd.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/mechanics/dnd.ts
import { useRef, useState, type PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from 'react';

/** The drop target under a point: the nearest element marked data-drop-id (the dragged item ignores the pointer). */
export function dropTargetAt(x: number, y: number): string | null {
  const els = typeof document.elementsFromPoint === 'function' ? document.elementsFromPoint(x, y) : [];
  for (const el of els) {
    const target = el.closest('[data-drop-id]');
    if (target) return target.getAttribute('data-drop-id');
  }
  return null;
}

/** A pointer that moves this far is a drag, not a tap. */
const DRAG_PX = 10;

export interface Dnd {
  /** the item picked by a tap, waiting for a tap on a target */
  selected: string | null;
  itemProps(id: string, enabled?: boolean): {
    'data-drag-id': string;
    className: string;
    onPointerDown(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>): void;
    onPointerMove(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>): void;
    onPointerUp(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>): void;
    onPointerCancel(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>): void;
    onClick(): void;
  };
  targetProps(id: string): { 'data-drop-id': string; onClick(): void };
}

/**
 * Drag an item onto a target, or tap the item and then the target (spec §11: tap and drag only). While dragged the
 * item follows the finger; let go anywhere else and it glides back (draggable's transition in mechanics.css).
 */
export function useDnd(options: { onDrop: (itemId: string, targetId: string) => void; onPick?: (itemId: string) => void }): Dnd {
  const opts = useRef(options);
  opts.current = options;
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const drag = useRef<{ id: string; el: HTMLElement; x0: number; y0: number; moved: boolean; pointerId: number } | null>(null);
  const wasDrag = useRef(false);

  const release = (drop: boolean, e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
    const d = drag.current;
    drag.current = null;
    if (!d || d.pointerId !== e.pointerId) return;
    d.el.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    d.el.style.transform = '';
    if (!d.moved) return;
    wasDrag.current = true;
    const target = drop ? dropTargetAt(e.clientX, e.clientY) : null;
    if (target) opts.current.onDrop(d.id, target);
  };

  return {
    selected,
    itemProps: (id, enabled = true) => ({
      'data-drag-id': id,
      className: `draggable${selected === id ? ' is-selected' : ''}`,
      onPointerDown(e) {
        if (!enabled) return;
        e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
        drag.current = { id, el: e.currentTarget, x0: e.clientX, y0: e.clientY, moved: false, pointerId: e.pointerId };
        wasDrag.current = false;
        opts.current.onPick?.(id);
      },
      onPointerMove(e) {
        const d = drag.current;
        if (!d || d.pointerId !== e.pointerId) return;
        const dx = e.clientX - d.x0;
        const dy = e.clientY - d.y0;
        if (!d.moved && Math.hypot(dx, dy) < DRAG_PX) return;
        if (!d.moved) setSelected(null);
        d.moved = true;
        d.el.classList.add('is-dragging');
        d.el.style.transform = `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px) scale(1.06)`;
      },
      onPointerUp: (e) => release(true, e),
      onPointerCancel: (e) => release(false, e),
      onClick() {
        if (wasDrag.current) {
          wasDrag.current = false;
          return;
        }
        if (!enabled) return;
        if (!drag.current) opts.current.onPick?.(id); // a click without pointer events (keyboard, tests)
        setSelected((s) => (s === id ? null : id));
      },
    }),
    targetProps: (id) => ({
      'data-drop-id': id,
      onClick() {
        if (selected === null) return;
        setSelected(null);
        opts.current.onDrop(selected, id);
      },
    }),
  };
}
```

**File: `src/app/testing/dom.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/testing/dom.ts
// Playing drag mechanics the tap way in tests (tap the item, then where it goes): jsdom has no layout to drag across.
import { fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';

/** An item or target by its id (data-drag-id or data-drop-id). */
export const byId = (c: HTMLElement, id: string): HTMLElement | null =>
  c.querySelector(`[data-drag-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"], [data-drop-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`);

export const target = (c: HTMLElement, id: string): HTMLElement => {
  const el = c.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-drop-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`);
  if (!el) throw new Error(`no target ${id}`);
  return el;
};

/** Tap an item, then a target. */
export function put(c: HTMLElement, itemId: string, targetId: string): void {
  const item = byId(c, itemId);
  if (!item) throw new Error(`no item ${itemId}`);
  fireEvent.click(item);
  fireEvent.click(target(c, targetId));
}
```

### Task 17: Mechanics: sort-into-bins and sequence

Spec §6 #8 and #10. Sorting (big and small baskets; fruit and vegetables): a wrong bin bounces the item back and its bin glows; a second miss with the same item and Buddy puts it in, and the child carries on. Sequence (the morning and bedtime routines): a card on the wrong slot bounces back and its slot glows; a second miss and Buddy places it. Misses count per item here (`stepFlow`'s key is the item), so one slip each on two items is two hints, not a demonstration — spec §4.4 written for rounds with one answer; Task 27 records the reading in the spec. A wrong placement is recorded as `item>target` (the tomato in the fruit basket), not the target alone, so the parent corner can name which item went where; the attempt record's comment says so.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/mechanics/SortIntoBins.tsx`, `src/app/mechanics/Sequence.tsx`
- Modify: `src/engine/progress/types.ts` (a comment)
- Test: `src/app/mechanics/sort.test.tsx`

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/sort.test.tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, render } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { SHOW_MS } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { byId, put, target } from '../testing/dom';
import { mechanicProps, roundOf } from '../testing/fakes';
import { Sequence } from './Sequence';
import { SortIntoBins } from './SortIntoBins';

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

describe('SortIntoBins', () => {
  it('puts each item in its bin; the round ends correct with every item sorted', () => {
    const round = roundOf('healthy-food.sort', 9, 2);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'bins') throw new Error('bins');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container } = render(<SortIntoBins {...props} />);
    for (const i of round.payload.items) put(container, i.id, i.binId);
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-drag-id]')).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, wrongIds: [] }]);
  });

  it('a wrong bin bounces the item back and its bin glows; a second miss with it and Buddy puts it in (shown)', () => {
    const round = roundOf('big-small.sort', 2, 1);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'bins') throw new Error('bins');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container } = render(<SortIntoBins {...props} />);
    const [first, ...rest] = round.payload.items;
    const wrongBin = first!.binId === 'big' ? 'small' : 'big';
    put(container, first!.id, wrongBin);
    expect(byId(container, first!.id)).not.toBeNull();
    expect(target(container, first!.binId).className).toContain('cue-hint');
    put(container, first!.id, wrongBin);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(SHOW_MS));
    expect(byId(container, first!.id)).toBeNull(); // Buddy put it in
    for (const i of rest) put(container, i.id, i.binId);
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'shown', hints: 1, wrongIds: [`${first!.id}>${wrongBin}`, `${first!.id}>${wrongBin}`] }]); // which item went where
  });
});

describe('Sequence', () => {
  it('fills the slots in order; a card on the wrong slot gets a hint, and the round is corrected', () => {
    const round = roundOf('good-habits.order', 1, 2);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'order') throw new Error('order');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container } = render(<Sequence {...props} />);
    const order = round.payload.correctOrder;
    put(container, order[1]!, 'slot:0');
    expect(target(container, 'slot:1').className).toContain('cue-hint');
    order.forEach((id, i) => put(container, id, `slot:${i}`));
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'corrected', hints: 1, wrongIds: [`${order[1]}>slot:0`] }]);
    expect([...round.payload.correctOrder]).toEqual(order); // the frozen round is untouched
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the two mechanics, and say what a wrong placement records**

**File: `src/app/mechanics/SortIntoBins.tsx`**

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/SortIntoBins.tsx
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { fillNames } from '../audio/speaker';
import { cueClass, SHOW_MS, useRoundFlow } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { useLater, useViewport } from '../ui/hooks';
import { ItemView } from '../ui/ItemView';
import { Picture } from '../ui/Picture';
import { useDnd } from './dnd';
import type { MechanicProps } from './types';
import './mechanics.css';

/**
 * Spec §6 #8: 2–3 bins, 4–8 items. A wrong bin bounces the item back with a hint (its bin glows); a second miss on the
 * same item and Buddy puts it in. Misses count per item.
 */
export function SortIntoBins({ round, deps, names, onEnd, onReact, quiet }: MechanicProps) {
  const p = round.payload;
  if (p.kind !== 'bins') throw new Error(`sort-into-bins cannot play a ${p.kind} round`);
  const later = useLater();
  const { w, h } = useViewport();
  const placed = useRef<Readonly<Record<string, string>>>({});
  const [, redraw] = useState(0);
  const waiting = p.items.filter((i) => !(i.id in placed.current));
  const binOf = (itemId: string) => p.items.find((i) => i.id === itemId)?.binId ?? '';
  const flow = useRoundFlow({
    deps, onEnd, onReact, quiet,
    targets: (key) => (key === null ? (waiting[0] ? [waiting[0].id, waiting[0].binId] : []) : [binOf(key)]),
  });

  const place = (itemId: string) => {
    if (itemId in placed.current) return;
    placed.current = { ...placed.current, [itemId]: binOf(itemId) };
    redraw((n) => n + 1);
    if (Object.keys(placed.current).length === p.items.length) flow.done();
    else flow.step();
  };

  const dnd = useDnd({
    onDrop(itemId, binId) {
      if (flow.ended || itemId in placed.current || !p.bins.some((b) => b.id === binId)) return;
      flow.touch();
      if (binOf(itemId) === binId) place(itemId);
      else if (flow.miss(itemId, `${itemId}>${binId}`) === 'show') later(() => place(itemId), SHOW_MS);
    },
    onPick: () => flow.touch(),
  });

  const size = Math.round(Math.min((w - 160) / Math.min(p.items.length, 6) - 36, h * 0.2, 150));
  const binSize = Math.round(Math.min(h * 0.22, 170));
  return (
    <div className="stage">
      <div className="tray">
        {waiting.map((item) => (
          <button key={item.id} type="button" aria-label={item.label} {...dnd.itemProps(item.id, !flow.ended)}>
            <span className={`option ${cueClass(flow.cue, item.id)}`} style={{ width: size + 20, height: size + 20 }}>
              <ItemView item={item} size={size} />
            </span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="row">
        {p.bins.map((bin) => (
          <div key={bin.id} className={`drop ${cueClass(flow.cue, bin.id)}`} {...dnd.targetProps(bin.id)} role="button" aria-label={fillNames(bin.label, names)}>
            {bin.asset && <Picture asset={bin.asset} label="" size={binSize} />}
            <span className="drop__label">{fillNames(bin.label, names)}</span>
            <div className="drop__inside">
              {p.items.filter((i) => placed.current[i.id] === bin.id).map((i) => <ItemView key={i.id} item={i} size={48} />)}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

**File: `src/app/mechanics/Sequence.tsx`**

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/Sequence.tsx
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { cueClass, SHOW_MS, useRoundFlow } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { artUrl } from '../ui/art';
import { useLater, useViewport } from '../ui/hooks';
import { Picture } from '../ui/Picture';
import { tiltFor } from '../ui/tilt';
import { useDnd } from './dnd';
import type { MechanicProps } from './types';
import './mechanics.css';

const slotId = (i: number) => `slot:${i}`;

/**
 * Spec §6 #10: 3–5 cards into ordered slots. A card dropped on the wrong slot bounces back with a hint (its slot
 * glows); a second miss with that card and Buddy puts it in its place. The round's correctOrder is never reordered.
 */
export function Sequence({ round, deps, onEnd, onReact, quiet }: MechanicProps) {
  const p = round.payload;
  if (p.kind !== 'order') throw new Error(`sequence cannot play a ${p.kind} round`);
  const later = useLater();
  const { w, h } = useViewport();
  const filled = useRef<readonly string[]>([]); // card ids placed, in any order
  const [, redraw] = useState(0);
  const slotOf = (cardId: string) => p.correctOrder.indexOf(cardId);
  const nextEmpty = p.correctOrder.findIndex((id) => !filled.current.includes(id));
  const flow = useRoundFlow({
    deps, onEnd, onReact, quiet,
    targets: (key) => (key === null ? (nextEmpty >= 0 ? [p.correctOrder[nextEmpty] as string, slotId(nextEmpty)] : []) : [slotId(slotOf(key))]),
  });

  const place = (cardId: string) => {
    if (filled.current.includes(cardId)) return;
    filled.current = [...filled.current, cardId];
    redraw((n) => n + 1);
    if (filled.current.length === p.correctOrder.length) flow.done();
    else flow.step();
  };

  const dnd = useDnd({
    onDrop(cardId, target) {
      if (flow.ended || filled.current.includes(cardId) || !target.startsWith('slot:')) return;
      const slot = Number(target.slice(5));
      if (filled.current.includes(p.correctOrder[slot] ?? '')) return; // that slot is already done
      flow.touch();
      if (slotOf(cardId) === slot) place(cardId);
      else if (flow.miss(cardId, `${cardId}>${target}`) === 'show') later(() => place(cardId), SHOW_MS);
    },
    onPick: () => flow.touch(),
  });

  const n = p.correctOrder.length;
  const size = Math.round(Math.min((w - 160) / n - 70, h * 0.2, 150));
  const card = (id: string) => p.items.find((i) => i.id === id);
  return (
    <div className="stage">
      <div className="tray">
        {p.items.filter((i) => !filled.current.includes(i.id)).map((item) => (
          <button key={item.id} type="button" aria-label={item.label} {...dnd.itemProps(item.id, !flow.ended)}>
            <span className={`option ${cueClass(flow.cue, item.id)}`} style={{ width: size + 24, rotate: tiltFor(item.id) }}>
              <Picture asset={item.asset} label="" size={size} />
              <span className="option__label">{item.label}</span>
            </span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="row" style={{ gap: 10 }}>
        {p.correctOrder.map((id, i) => {
          const done = filled.current.includes(id);
          const placedCard = done ? card(id) : undefined;
          return (
            <div key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
              {i > 0 && <img src={artUrl('ui/back.svg')} alt="" style={{ width: 36, transform: 'scaleX(-1)', opacity: 0.6 }} />}
              <div className={`drop ${cueClass(flow.cue, slotId(i))}`} style={{ minWidth: size + 30, minHeight: size + 40 }} {...dnd.targetProps(slotId(i))} role="button" aria-label={`place ${i + 1}`}>
                <span className="drop__label">{i + 1}</span>
                {placedCard && <Picture asset={placedCard.asset} label={placedCard.label} size={size} />}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 18: Mechanics: give-to-buddy

Spec §6 #9: drag items onto Buddy or onto the plate, and Buddy reacts to each (yum; yuck at junk — a reaction to the food, never sadness at a wrong answer). The healthy plate ends when every healthy food is on it; junk is a miss for the round, and the second has Buddy put the rest on. Give-a-number (Plan 1A's notes) takes **any** `count` of the interchangeable copies: the child gives, can take one back with a tap, and taps ✓ — the spec does not say how a child says "that's all", and a count only counts if the child can stop. A wrong count is a miss (`n:<count>` among the wrong picks), and its hint numbers what was given, so the child can count along.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/mechanics/GiveToBuddy.tsx`
- Test: `src/app/mechanics/give.test.tsx`

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/give.test.tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, fireEvent, render } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { put } from '../testing/dom';
import { mechanicProps, roundOf } from '../testing/fakes';
import { GiveToBuddy } from './GiveToBuddy';

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

describe('GiveToBuddy', () => {
  it('healthy plate: the healthy foods go on, junk gets a "yuck" and a hint', () => {
    const round = roundOf('healthy-food.plate', 4, 1);
    const p = round.payload;
    if (p.kind !== 'give') throw new Error('give');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container } = render(<GiveToBuddy {...props} />);
    const junk = p.items.find((i) => !p.correctIds.includes(i.id))!;
    put(container, junk.id, 'target');
    expect(rec.said).toEqual(['fb.try_again']);
    for (const id of p.correctIds) put(container, id, 'target');
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'corrected', hints: 1, wrongIds: [junk.id] }]);
  });

  it('a second give soon after the first gets its own yum, kept until 1.2 s after it', () => {
    const round = roundOf('counting.give', 8, 1);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'give') throw new Error('give');
    const { props } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container } = render(<GiveToBuddy {...props} />);
    const [a, b] = round.payload.items;
    put(container, a!.id, 'target');
    const first = container.querySelector('svg.buddy');
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(800));
    put(container, b!.id, 'target');
    const second = container.querySelector('svg.buddy');
    expect(second).not.toBe(first); // a new Buddy: the nod plays again
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(600)); // past the first give's 1.2 s
    expect(container.querySelector('svg.buddy')!.getAttribute('class')).toContain('buddy--yum');
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(700));
    expect(container.querySelector('svg.buddy')!.getAttribute('class')).toContain('buddy--idle');
  });

  it('give-count: any n copies and ✓ is right first time', () => {
    const round = roundOf('counting.give', 8, 1);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'give') throw new Error('give');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container, getByLabelText } = render(<GiveToBuddy {...props} />);
    const n = round.payload.count!;
    round.payload.items.slice(-n).forEach((i) => put(container, i.id, 'target')); // not the ids correctIds suggests
    fireEvent.click(getByLabelText('Done'));
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, wrongIds: [] }]);
  });

  it('give-count: a wrong count is a miss that numbers what was given; taking one back fixes it', () => {
    const round = roundOf('counting.give', 8, 1);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'give') throw new Error('give');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container, getByLabelText } = render(<GiveToBuddy {...props} />);
    const n = round.payload.count!;
    const extra = round.payload.items.slice(0, n + 1);
    extra.forEach((i) => put(container, i.id, 'target'));
    fireEvent.click(getByLabelText('Done'));
    expect(rec.said).toEqual(['fb.try_again']);
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('.badge')).toHaveLength(n + 1);
    fireEvent.click(container.querySelector('.drop__inside button')!); // take one back
    fireEvent.click(getByLabelText('Done'));
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'corrected', hints: 1, wrongIds: [`n:${n + 1}`] }]);
  });
});
```
