- [ ] **Step 3: Write the mechanic**

**File: `src/app/mechanics/GiveToBuddy.tsx`**

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/GiveToBuddy.tsx
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { fillNames } from '../audio/speaker';
import { Buddy, type BuddyMove } from '../buddy/Buddy';
import { cueClass, SETTLE_MS, SHOW_MS, useRoundFlow } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { artUrl } from '../ui/art';
import { useLater, useViewport } from '../ui/hooks';
import { ItemView } from '../ui/ItemView';
import { Picture } from '../ui/Picture';
import { useDnd } from './dnd';
import type { MechanicProps } from './types';
import './mechanics.css';

const TARGET = 'target';

/**
 * Spec §6 #9: drag items onto Buddy or onto a target (the plate); Buddy reacts to each (yum / yuck).
 * Without `count`, the correctIds items are the ones to give, and it ends when all are given.
 * With `count` (give-count), the items are copies and any `count` of them is right: the child gives, can take one
 * back with a tap, and taps ✓ — a wrong count is a miss, and the hint numbers the ones given (count with me).
 */
export function GiveToBuddy({ round, deps, names, look, onEnd, onReact, quiet }: MechanicProps) {
  const p = round.payload;
  if (p.kind !== 'give') throw new Error(`give-to-buddy cannot play a ${p.kind} round`);
  const later = useLater();
  const { w, h } = useViewport();
  const given = useRef<readonly string[]>([]);
  const [, redraw] = useState(0);
  // `beat` counts reactions: it keys Buddy, so a second yum plays again, and only the latest one goes back to idle
  const [buddy, setBuddy] = useState<{ move: BuddyMove; beat: number }>({ move: 'idle', beat: 0 });
  const beat = useRef(0);
  const buddyMove = buddy.move;
  const [numbered, setNumbered] = useState(false);
  // once Buddy shows the answer the round is decided: drops, take-backs and ✓ wait for it to end
  const showing = useRef(false);
  // ✓ again at once with the same count is the same answer (a bounce, a double tap), not a second miss
  const settling = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const counting = p.count !== undefined;
  const left = p.correctIds.filter((id) => !given.current.includes(id));
  const flow = useRoundFlow({
    deps, onEnd, onReact, quiet,
    targets: (key) => (counting ? (key === null ? [TARGET] : [...given.current]) : left),
  });

  const react = (move: BuddyMove) => {
    const mine = ++beat.current;
    setBuddy({ move, beat: mine });
    later(() => {
      if (beat.current === mine) setBuddy({ move: 'idle', beat: mine });
    }, 1200);
  };
  const setGiven = (next: readonly string[]) => {
    given.current = next;
    redraw((n) => n + 1);
  };

  const give = (itemId: string) => {
    if (given.current.includes(itemId)) return;
    setGiven([...given.current, itemId]);
    react('yum');
    if (!counting && p.correctIds.every((id) => given.current.includes(id))) flow.done();
    else flow.step();
  };

  const dnd = useDnd({
    onDrop(itemId, target) {
      if (flow.ended || showing.current || target !== TARGET || given.current.includes(itemId)) return;
      flow.touch();
      if (counting || p.correctIds.includes(itemId)) {
        give(itemId);
        return;
      }
      react('yuck');
      if (flow.miss('round', itemId) === 'show') {
        showing.current = true;
        left.forEach((id, i) => later(() => give(id), SHOW_MS + i * 450));
      }
    },
    onPick: () => flow.touch(),
  });

  const takeBack = (itemId: string) => {
    if (flow.ended || showing.current || !counting) return;
    flow.touch();
    setGiven(given.current.filter((id) => id !== itemId));
  };

  const check = () => {
    const answer = `n:${given.current.length}`;
    if (flow.ended || showing.current || settling.current === answer || p.count === undefined) return;
    flow.touch();
    if (given.current.length === p.count) {
      flow.done();
      return;
    }
    setNumbered(true);
    settling.current = answer;
    later(() => {
      if (settling.current === answer) settling.current = null;
    }, SETTLE_MS);
    if (flow.miss('round', answer) === 'show') {
      showing.current = true;
      later(() => setGiven(p.items.slice(0, p.count).map((i) => i.id)), SHOW_MS);
      later(() => flow.done(), SHOW_MS + 900);
    }
  };

  const size = Math.round(Math.min((w - 200) / Math.min(p.items.length, 6) - 34, h * 0.17, 130));
  const targetSize = Math.round(Math.min(h * 0.36, 280));
  const item = (id: string) => p.items.find((i) => i.id === id);
  return (
    <div className="stage">
      <div className="tray">
        {p.items.filter((i) => !given.current.includes(i.id)).map((it) => (
          <button key={it.id} type="button" aria-label={it.label} {...dnd.itemProps(it.id, !flow.ended)}>
            <span className={`option ${cueClass(flow.cue, it.id)}`} style={{ width: size + 18, height: size + 18 }}>
              <ItemView item={it} size={size} />
            </span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="row">
        <div className={`drop ${cueClass(flow.cue, TARGET)}`} {...dnd.targetProps(TARGET)} role="button" aria-label={fillNames(p.target.label, names)} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 16 }}>
          {p.target.asset
            ? <><Buddy key={buddy.beat} {...look} move={buddyMove} expression={buddyMove === 'yum' ? 'happy' : 'neutral'} size={targetSize * 0.8} /><Picture asset={p.target.asset} label="" size={targetSize * 0.7} /></>
            : <Buddy key={buddy.beat} {...look} move={buddyMove} expression={buddyMove === 'yum' ? 'happy' : 'neutral'} size={targetSize} />}
          <div className="drop__inside" style={{ maxWidth: 220 }}>
            {given.current.map((id, i) => {
              const it = item(id);
              return it && (
                <button key={id} type="button" className={`option ${cueClass(flow.cue, id)}`} style={{ padding: 4, position: 'relative' }} aria-label={it.label} onClick={() => takeBack(id)}>
                  {numbered && <span className="badge">{i + 1}</span>}
                  <ItemView item={it} size={56} />
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
        {counting && given.current.length > 0 && (
          <button type="button" className="done-button" aria-label="Done" onClick={check}>
            <img src={artUrl('ui/done.svg')} alt="" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 19: Mechanics: drag-to-match

Spec §6 #7: two columns; drag from an item to its partner, from either side, and a line is drawn that stays — or tap one, then the other. A wrong partner lights up the right one; a second miss from the same item and Buddy draws its line. A wrong partner is recorded as `item>partner`. Matching words (rhymes, capital and small letters) says each picture's or letter's name as it is picked up: a rhyme is heard, not seen. Counted groups say nothing (their name is not the number). The lines are measured from the cards after each render and stored only when they move.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/mechanics/DragToMatch.tsx`
- Test: `src/app/mechanics/match.test.tsx`

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/match.test.tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, fireEvent, render } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { SHOW_MS } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { target } from '../testing/dom';
import { mechanicProps, roundOf } from '../testing/fakes';
import { DragToMatch } from './DragToMatch';

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

describe('DragToMatch', () => {
  it('matches each pair, says the words of a rhyme round, and ends correct', () => {
    const round = roundOf('rhyming.match', 6, 2);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'pairs') throw new Error('pairs');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container } = render(<DragToMatch {...props} />);
    for (const [a, b] of round.payload.pairs) {
      fireEvent.click(target(container, b)); // either side first
      fireEvent.click(target(container, a));
    }
    expect(rec.words.length).toBe(round.payload.pairs.length);
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, wrongIds: [] }]);
  });

  it('a wrong partner lights the right one; a second miss from that item and Buddy draws its line', () => {
    const round = roundOf('capital-small.match', 2, 3);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'pairs') throw new Error('pairs');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container } = render(<DragToMatch {...props} />);
    const [[a, b], [, other]] = round.payload.pairs as [readonly [string, string], readonly [string, string]];
    fireEvent.click(target(container, a));
    fireEvent.click(target(container, other));
    expect(target(container, b).className).toContain('cue-hint');
    fireEvent.click(target(container, a));
    fireEvent.click(target(container, other));
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(SHOW_MS));
    expect(target(container, a).getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    for (const [l, r] of round.payload.pairs.slice(1)) {
      fireEvent.click(target(container, l));
      fireEvent.click(target(container, r));
    }
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'shown', hints: 1, wrongIds: [`${a}>${other}`, `${a}>${other}`] }]);
  });

  it('a tap on a picture says its name once', () => {
    const round = roundOf('rhyming.match', 6, 2);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'pairs') throw new Error('pairs');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container } = render(<DragToMatch {...props} />);
    const item = target(container, round.payload.pairs[0]![0]);
    fireEvent.pointerDown(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 10, clientY: 10 });
    fireEvent.pointerMove(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 13, clientY: 12 }); // a wobble is still a tap
    fireEvent.pointerUp(item, { pointerId: 1, clientX: 13, clientY: 12 });
    fireEvent.click(item);
    expect(rec.words.length).toBe(1);
    expect(item.className).toContain('is-selected');
  });

  it('says no words for counted groups', () => {
    const round = roundOf('counting.match', 5, 2);
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    const { container } = render(<DragToMatch {...props} />);
    if (round.payload.kind !== 'pairs') throw new Error('pairs');
    fireEvent.click(target(container, round.payload.pairs[0]![0]));
    expect(rec.words).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/DragToMatch.tsx
import { useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type PointerEvent as ReactPointerEvent } from 'react';
import { cueClass, SHOW_MS, useRoundFlow } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { useLater, useViewport } from '../ui/hooks';
import { ItemView } from '../ui/ItemView';
import { tiltFor } from '../ui/tilt';
import { DRAG_PX, dropTargetAt } from './dnd';
import type { MechanicProps } from './types';
import './mechanics.css';

interface Line { x1: number; y1: number; x2: number; y2: number }

/**
 * Spec §6 #7: two columns, 2–4 pairs. Drag from an item to its partner (either side) and a line is drawn, which stays;
 * or tap one, then the other. A wrong partner gives a hint (the right one glows); a second miss from the same item and
 * Buddy draws its line. Matching words (rhymes, letters) says each picture's name as it is picked up.
 */
export function DragToMatch({ round, deps, onEnd, onReact, quiet }: MechanicProps) {
  const p = round.payload;
  if (p.kind !== 'pairs') throw new Error(`drag-to-match cannot play a ${p.kind} round`);
  const later = useLater();
  const { w, h } = useViewport();
  const matched = useRef<readonly string[]>([]); // left ids
  // a pair Buddy is showing waits for him to draw its line (no third miss, no race with his hand); the others stay free
  const showing = useRef<readonly string[]>([]); // left ids
  const [, redraw] = useState(0);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [band, setBand] = useState<Line | null>(null);
  const [lines, setLines] = useState<readonly Line[]>([]);
  const box = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const els = useRef(new Map<string, HTMLElement>());
  const drag = useRef<{ id: string; pointerId: number; x0: number; y0: number; moved: boolean } | null>(null);
  // how the last press went: the click that follows it is that same press, its word said (a drag is no tap)
  const press = useRef<'tap' | 'drag' | null>(null);

  const isLeft = (id: string) => p.left.some((i) => i.id === id);
  const partner = (id: string) => {
    const pair = p.pairs.find(([a, b]) => a === id || b === id);
    return pair ? (pair[0] === id ? pair[1] : pair[0]) : '';
  };
  const leftOf = (id: string) => (isLeft(id) ? id : partner(id));
  const done = (id: string) => matched.current.includes(leftOf(id));
  // matched, or about to be drawn by Buddy: neither end takes a press or a line
  const busy = (id: string) => done(id) || showing.current.includes(leftOf(id));
  const speaksNames = p.left.every((i) => i.count === undefined);
  const firstOpen = p.left.find((i) => !matched.current.includes(i.id));
  const flow = useRoundFlow({
    deps, onEnd, onReact, quiet,
    targets: (key) => (key === null ? (firstOpen ? [firstOpen.id, partner(firstOpen.id)] : []) : [partner(key)]),
  });

  const match = (leftId: string) => {
    if (matched.current.includes(leftId)) return;
    matched.current = [...matched.current, leftId];
    redraw((n) => n + 1);
    if (matched.current.length === p.pairs.length) flow.done();
    else flow.step();
  };

  const connect = (from: string, to: string) => {
    if (flow.ended || busy(from) || busy(to) || isLeft(from) === isLeft(to)) return;
    flow.touch();
    if (partner(from) === to) match(leftOf(from));
    else if (flow.miss(from, `${from}>${to}`) === 'show') {
      showing.current = [...showing.current, leftOf(from)];
      redraw((n) => n + 1);
      later(() => match(leftOf(from)), SHOW_MS);
    }
  };

  const centre = (id: string, side: 'left' | 'right'): { x: number; y: number } | null => {
    const el = els.current.get(id);
    const origin = box.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!el || !origin) return null;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    return { x: (side === 'left' ? r.right : r.left) - origin.left, y: r.top + r.height / 2 - origin.top };
  };

  // the matched pairs' lines, measured after every render (a match, a resize); state changes only when they move
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const next = matched.current.flatMap((l) => {
      const a = centre(l, 'left');
      const b = centre(partner(l), 'right');
      return a && b ? [{ x1: a.x, y1: a.y, x2: b.x, y2: b.y }] : [];
    });
    setLines((prev) => (JSON.stringify(prev) === JSON.stringify(next) ? prev : next));
  });

  const pick = (id: string, wordToo: boolean) => {
    flow.touch();
    if (wordToo && speaksNames) void deps.speaker.word((p.left.find((i) => i.id === id) ?? p.right.find((i) => i.id === id))?.lineId ?? '');
  };

  const pointer = (id: string) => ({
    onPointerDown(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>) {
      if (flow.ended || busy(id)) return;
      e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
      drag.current = { id, pointerId: e.pointerId, x0: e.clientX, y0: e.clientY, moved: false };
      press.current = 'tap';
      pick(id, true);
    },
    onPointerMove(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>) {
      const d = drag.current;
      const origin = box.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (!d || d.pointerId !== e.pointerId || !origin) return;
      if (!d.moved && Math.hypot(e.clientX - d.x0, e.clientY - d.y0) < DRAG_PX) return; // a tiny wobble is a tap
      const from = centre(d.id, isLeft(d.id) ? 'left' : 'right');
      if (!from) return;
      d.moved = true;
      setBand({ x1: from.x, y1: from.y, x2: e.clientX - origin.left, y2: e.clientY - origin.top });
    },
    onPointerUp(e: ReactPointerEvent<HTMLElement>) {
      const d = drag.current;
      drag.current = null;
      setBand(null);
      if (!d || !d.moved) return;
      press.current = 'drag';
      const target = dropTargetAt(e.clientX, e.clientY);
      if (target && target !== d.id) connect(d.id, target);
    },
    onPointerCancel() {
      drag.current = null;
      setBand(null);
    },
    onClick() {
      const pressed = press.current;
      press.current = null;
      if (pressed === 'drag' || flow.ended || busy(id)) return;
      if (selected && isLeft(selected) !== isLeft(id)) {
        setSelected(null);
        connect(selected, id);
        return;
      }
      if (pressed === null) pick(id, true); // a click with no press before it: the keyboard, tests
      setSelected((s) => (s === id ? null : id));
    },
  });

  const rows = Math.max(p.left.length, p.right.length);
  const size = Math.round(Math.min((h - 220) / rows - 30, w * 0.16, 150));
  const column = (items: typeof p.left, side: 'left' | 'right') => (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 18, alignItems: side === 'left' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start' }}>
      {items.map((item) => (
        <button
          key={item.id}
          ref={(el) => { if (el) els.current.set(item.id, el); else els.current.delete(item.id); }}
          type="button"
          data-drop-id={item.id}
          aria-label={item.label}
          aria-pressed={done(item.id)}
          className={`option draggable ${cueClass(flow.cue, item.id)} ${done(item.id) ? 'is-right' : ''} ${selected === item.id ? 'is-selected' : ''}`}
          style={{ width: size + 24, height: size + 24, rotate: tiltFor(item.id), touchAction: 'none' }}
          {...pointer(item.id)}
        >
          <ItemView item={item} size={size} />
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="stage">
      <div ref={box} style={{ position: 'relative', display: 'flex', gap: 'clamp(90px, 22vw, 320px)' }}>
        <svg style={{ position: 'absolute', inset: 0, width: '100%', height: '100%', overflow: 'visible', pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
          {lines.map((l, i) => <line key={i} {...l} stroke="#5c9e31" strokeWidth={8} strokeLinecap="round" />)}
          {band && <line {...band} stroke="#3d2f27" strokeWidth={6} strokeDasharray="2 14" strokeLinecap="round" />}
        </svg>
        {column(p.left, 'left')}
        {column(p.right, 'right')}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 20: Mechanics: scene-then-choose, and the registry

Spec §6 #22: a short vignette, narrated (the narration ends with the question, said once), then two or three choices. The vignettes are compositions in code, not files, because Buddy acts in them in the child's colours; each fills the frame (the set's scene could contradict it). A feeling chosen right, and Buddy acts it out. In the kindness scenes an unkind choice says its reaction line and shows the friend's sad face — Buddy never acts it out (Plan 1A's notes) — and the kind one makes the friend smile. A scene can have two right answers (a broken toy: angry or sad), so choices are judged by `correct`. The registry maps each M1 mechanic id to its component; a mechanic not built yet throws, which the game screen turns into a graceful return to the Map (Task 21). A sweep plays every M1 template's last round at every level.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/scenario/vignettes.ts`, `src/app/scenario/Vignette.tsx`, `src/app/scenario/vignette.css`, `src/app/mechanics/SceneThenChoose.tsx`, `src/app/mechanics/Mechanic.tsx`
- Test: `src/app/mechanics/scene.test.tsx`

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/scene.test.tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { SHOW_MS } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { VIGNETTES, WATCH_MS } from '../scenario/vignettes';
import { mechanicProps, pack, roundOf } from '../testing/fakes';
import { isPlayable, Mechanic } from './Mechanic';
import { SceneThenChoose } from './SceneThenChoose';

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

/** A scenario round of the topic whose scenario has this id, from some seed. */
function scenario(templateId: string, scenarioId: string) {
  for (let seed = 0; seed < 200; seed++) {
    const round = roundOf(templateId, seed);
    if (round.payload.kind === 'scenario' && round.payload.scenarioId === scenarioId) return round;
  }
  throw new Error(`no seed draws ${scenarioId}`);
}

describe('SceneThenChoose', () => {
  it('plays the vignette, then offers the choices; a right feeling: Buddy acts it out and cheers', () => {
    const round = scenario('feelings.scene', 'sc.ice-cream-fell');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    render(<SceneThenChoose {...props} />);
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('sad')).toBeNull();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(WATCH_MS));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('sad'));
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('img').some((el) => el.getAttribute('class')?.includes('buddy--sad'))).toBe(true);
    expect(rec.said).toEqual(['fb.correct']);
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'correct', hints: 0, wrongIds: [] }]);
  });

  it('kindness: an unkind choice says its reaction and shows the friend sad; the kind one, its reaction, and the friend smiles', () => {
    const round = scenario('kind-fair.scene', 'sc.friend-crying');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    render(<SceneThenChoose {...props} />);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(WATCH_MS));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('laugh at them'));
    expect(rec.said).toEqual(['react.unkind']);
    expect((screen.getByAltText('your friend') as HTMLImageElement).src).toContain('face-sad');
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('img').some((el) => el.getAttribute('class')?.includes('buddy--sad'))).toBe(false); // Buddy never acts it out
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('give a hug'));
    expect(rec.said).toEqual(['react.unkind', 'react.kind']);
    expect((screen.getByAltText('your friend') as HTMLImageElement).src).toContain('face-happy');
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'corrected', hints: 1, wrongIds: ['laugh'] }]);
  });

  it('accepts either right answer when a scene has two (a broken toy: angry or sad)', () => {
    const round = scenario('feelings.scene', 'sc.toy-broken');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    render(<SceneThenChoose {...props} />);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(WATCH_MS));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('angry'));
    expect(rec.ends[0]?.outcome).toBe('correct');
  });

  it('after two misses Buddy shows the answer and the round is shown', () => {
    const round = scenario('feelings.scene', 'sc.loud-thunder');
    const { props, rec } = mechanicProps(round);
    render(<SceneThenChoose {...props} />);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(WATCH_MS));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('happy'));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('sleepy'));
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(SHOW_MS));
    expect(rec.ends).toEqual([{ outcome: 'shown', hints: 1, wrongIds: ['happy', 'sleepy'] }]);
  });
});

describe('the M1 mechanics', () => {
  it('every scenario has a vignette', () => {
    for (const s of pack.scenarios.values()) expect(VIGNETTES[s.vignette], s.id).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('play every M1 template\'s rounds at every level without throwing', () => {
    for (const template of pack.templates.values()) {
      expect(isPlayable(template.mechanic), template.id).toBe(true);
      for (const level of [1, 2, 3] as const) {
        const round = roundOf(template.id, 7, level, template.rounds - 1);
        const { unmount } = render(<Mechanic {...mechanicProps(round).props} />);
        act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(WATCH_MS));
        unmount();
      }
    }
  });
});
```

```ts
// src/app/scenario/vignettes.ts
/**
 * The scene-then-choose vignettes (spec §6 #22), keyed by the scenario's `vignette`. They are composed here rather than
 * drawn as files because Buddy acts in them, in the colour and accessory the child chose. Each fills the whole frame
 * (the set's background could contradict it: thunder over a sunny playground).
 */

/** The friend in the kindness scenes: a face whose mood follows the child's choice (sad after an unkind one). */
export type FriendMood = 'happy' | 'sad' | 'crying' | 'hurt';

export const FRIEND_FACES: Record<FriendMood, string> = {
  happy: 'parts/face-happy.svg',
  sad: 'parts/face-sad.svg',
  crying: 'parts/face-crying.svg',
  hurt: 'parts/face-hurt.svg',
};

export interface Actor {
  /** Buddy (the child's), the friend's face, or a picture */
  who: 'buddy' | 'friend' | 'art';
  asset?: string;
  /** centre, in % of the frame's width and height */
  x: number;
  y: number;
  /** height, in % of the frame's height */
  size: number;
  /** a short animation, from vignette.css */
  anim?: 'fall' | 'pop' | 'shake' | 'flash' | 'bob' | 'float' | 'tip';
  /** seconds before it starts */
  delay?: number;
}

export interface Vignette {
  backdrop: 'park' | 'party' | 'storm' | 'room';
  /** the friend's mood before the child chooses */
  friend?: FriendMood;
  actors: readonly Actor[];
}

/** How long the vignette plays before the choices appear. */
export const WATCH_MS = 4500;

export const VIGNETTES: Readonly<Record<string, Vignette>> = {
  'vignettes/ice-cream-fell.svg': {
    backdrop: 'park',
    actors: [
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/tree.svg', x: 12, y: 46, size: 60 },
      { who: 'buddy', x: 42, y: 56, size: 76 },
      { who: 'art', asset: 'objects/ice-cream.svg', x: 64, y: 84, size: 22, anim: 'fall', delay: 0.8 },
    ],
  },
  'vignettes/birthday-gift.svg': {
    backdrop: 'party',
    actors: [
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/balloon.svg', x: 12, y: 30, size: 34, anim: 'float' },
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/balloon.svg', x: 88, y: 26, size: 30, anim: 'float', delay: 0.6 },
      { who: 'buddy', x: 38, y: 56, size: 76 },
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/cake.svg', x: 82, y: 74, size: 26 },
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/gift.svg', x: 64, y: 72, size: 34, anim: 'pop', delay: 0.8 },
    ],
  },
  'vignettes/loud-thunder.svg': {
    backdrop: 'storm',
    actors: [
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/storm.svg', x: 70, y: 24, size: 38, anim: 'shake' },
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/lightning.svg', x: 74, y: 52, size: 26, anim: 'flash', delay: 0.6 },
      { who: 'buddy', x: 36, y: 58, size: 76 },
    ],
  },
  'vignettes/toy-broken.svg': {
    backdrop: 'room',
    actors: [
      { who: 'buddy', x: 30, y: 56, size: 76 },
      { who: 'art', asset: 'objects/toy-car.svg', x: 60, y: 80, size: 20, anim: 'tip', delay: 0.8 },
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/person.svg', x: 82, y: 56, size: 66 },
    ],
  },
  'vignettes/friend-crying.svg': {
    backdrop: 'park',
    friend: 'crying',
    actors: [
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/slide.svg', x: 20, y: 56, size: 58 },
      { who: 'friend', x: 62, y: 54, size: 44, anim: 'bob' },
    ],
  },
  'vignettes/one-biscuit.svg': {
    backdrop: 'room',
    friend: 'sad',
    actors: [
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/cookie.svg', x: 32, y: 60, size: 26, anim: 'pop', delay: 0.5 },
      { who: 'friend', x: 70, y: 52, size: 40 },
    ],
  },
  'vignettes/slide-queue.svg': {
    backdrop: 'park',
    friend: 'happy',
    actors: [
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/slide.svg', x: 82, y: 54, size: 60 },
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/person.svg', x: 58, y: 60, size: 52 },
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/person.svg', x: 44, y: 60, size: 52 },
      { who: 'friend', x: 28, y: 56, size: 30 },
    ],
  },
  'vignettes/friend-fell.svg': {
    backdrop: 'park',
    friend: 'hurt',
    actors: [
      { who: 'art', asset: 'parts/tree.svg', x: 16, y: 46, size: 60 },

};
```

**File: `src/app/scenario/Vignette.tsx`**

```tsx
// src/app/scenario/Vignette.tsx
import { Buddy, type BuddyExpression } from '../buddy/Buddy';
import type { BuddyLook } from '../mechanics/types';
import { Picture } from '../ui/Picture';
import { FRIEND_FACES, VIGNETTES, type FriendMood } from './vignettes';
import './vignette.css';

/** A vignette, full frame: Buddy with the given expression, the friend with the given mood. */
export function VignetteView({ vignette, look, buddy, friend, height }: { vignette: string; look: BuddyLook; buddy: BuddyExpression; friend?: FriendMood; height: number }) {
  const v = VIGNETTES[vignette];
  if (!v) throw new Error(`no vignette ${vignette}`);
  return (
    <div className={`vignette vignette--${v.backdrop} card`} style={{ height, width: height * 1.6 }}>
      {v.actors.map((a, i) => {
        const px = (height * a.size) / 100;
        const style = { left: `${a.x}%`, top: `${a.y}%`, height: px, animationDelay: `${a.delay ?? 0}s` };
        const anim = a.anim ? `vignette__actor--${a.anim}` : '';
        if (a.who === 'buddy') {
          return <div key={i} className={`vignette__actor ${anim}`} style={style}><Buddy {...look} expression={buddy} size={px} /></div>;
        }
        const asset = a.who === 'friend' ? FRIEND_FACES[friend ?? v.friend ?? 'happy'] : (a.asset as string);
        return <div key={i} className={`vignette__actor ${anim}`} style={style}><Picture asset={asset} label={a.who === 'friend' ? 'your friend' : ''} size={px} /></div>;
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css
/* src/app/scenario/vignette.css */
.vignette { position: relative; overflow: hidden; border-radius: 28px; flex: none; }
.vignette--park { background: linear-gradient(#d9eefa 0 62%, #cfe8b0 62%); }
.vignette--party { background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 30%, #fff5d6, #fde2c4 70%), #fde2c4; }
.vignette--storm { background: linear-gradient(#7d8ea3 0 62%, #9fb39a 62%); }
.vignette--room { background: linear-gradient(#f2e5d3 0 66%, #d9c2a7 66%); }
.vignette__actor { position: absolute; transform: translate(-50%, -50%); display: grid; place-items: center; }
.vignette__actor--fall { animation: v-fall 1.1s cubic-bezier(.5, 0, .8, .6) both; }
.vignette__actor--pop { animation: v-pop 0.6s cubic-bezier(.3, 1.6, .5, 1) both; }
.vignette__actor--shake { animation: v-shake 0.18s linear 8; }
.vignette__actor--flash { animation: v-flash 0.5s steps(2) 6 both; }
.vignette__actor--bob { animation: v-bob 0.9s ease-in-out infinite; }
.vignette__actor--float { animation: v-float 2.4s ease-in-out infinite; }
.vignette__actor--tip { animation: v-tip 0.8s ease-in both; }
@keyframes v-fall { from { transform: translate(-50%, -260%) rotate(0); } to { transform: translate(-50%, -50%) rotate(160deg); } }
@keyframes v-pop { from { transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0); } to { transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1); } }
@keyframes v-shake { 25% { transform: translate(-53%, -50%); } 75% { transform: translate(-47%, -50%); } }
@keyframes v-flash { from { opacity: 0; } to { opacity: 1; } }
@keyframes v-bob { 50% { transform: translate(-50%, -46%); } }
@keyframes v-float { 50% { transform: translate(-50%, -58%); } }
@keyframes v-tip { from { transform: translate(-50%, -50%) rotate(0); } to { transform: translate(-50%, -40%) rotate(-80deg); } }
```

**File: `src/app/mechanics/SceneThenChoose.tsx`**

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/SceneThenChoose.tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { isExpression, type BuddyExpression } from '../buddy/Buddy';
import { cueClass, SETTLE_MS, SHOW_MS, useRoundFlow } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { VignetteView } from '../scenario/Vignette';
import { VIGNETTES, WATCH_MS, type FriendMood } from '../scenario/vignettes';
import { useLater, useViewport } from '../ui/hooks';
import { Picture } from '../ui/Picture';
import { tiltFor } from '../ui/tilt';
import type { MechanicProps } from './types';
import './mechanics.css';

type Choice = { id: string; label: string; asset: string; correct: boolean; reactionLineId?: string };

/**
 * Spec §6 #22: a short vignette (narrated, ending with the question), then 2–3 choices. A feeling chosen right, Buddy
 * acts it out. In the kindness scenes an unkind choice shows the friend's sad face — never Buddy acting it out — and
 * says the choice's reaction line; a kind one makes the friend smile. Judged by `correct`, never by slot (a scene can
 * have two right answers).
 */
export function SceneThenChoose({ round, deps, look, onEnd, onReact, quiet }: MechanicProps) {
  const p = round.payload;
  if (p.kind !== 'scenario') throw new Error(`scene-then-choose cannot play a ${p.kind} round`);
  const later = useLater();
  const { w, h } = useViewport();
  const [watching, setWatching] = useState(true);
  const [buddy, setBuddy] = useState<BuddyExpression>('neutral');
  const [friend, setFriend] = useState<FriendMood | undefined>(VIGNETTES[p.vignette]?.friend);
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState<{ id: string; n: number } | null>(null);
  // one tap, one answer (as tap-the-one): no second miss at once on the card that just shook, nothing while Buddy shows
  const settling = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const showing = useRef(false);
  const right = p.choices.filter((c) => c.correct);
  const flow = useRoundFlow({ deps, targets: () => right.map((c) => c.id), onEnd, onReact, quiet });

  // the quiet spell runs from when the child can choose, not while the scene plays
  useEffect(() => later(() => { setWatching(false); flow.touch(); }, WATCH_MS), [later]);

  const actOut = (c: Choice) => {
    if (isExpression(c.id)) setBuddy(c.id);
    else setFriend('happy');
  };

  const tap = (c: Choice) => {
    if (flow.ended || picked !== null || showing.current || settling.current === c.id) return;
    flow.touch();
    if (c.correct) {
      setPicked(c.id);
      actOut(c);
      flow.done(c.reactionLineId);
      return;
    }
    setShake((s) => ({ id: c.id, n: (s?.n ?? 0) + 1 }));
    if (c.reactionLineId) setFriend('sad');
    settling.current = c.id;
    later(() => {
      if (settling.current === c.id) settling.current = null;
    }, SETTLE_MS);
    if (flow.miss('round', c.id, c.reactionLineId) === 'show') {
      showing.current = true;
      later(() => {
        const answer = right[0] as Choice;
        setPicked(answer.id);
        actOut(answer);
        flow.done();
      }, SHOW_MS);
    }
  };

  const frame = Math.round(Math.min(watching ? h * 0.62 : h * 0.42, (w - 80) / 1.6));
  const size = Math.round(Math.min(h * 0.18, (w - 160) / p.choices.length - 60, 150));
  return (
    <div className="stage">
      <VignetteView vignette={p.vignette} look={look} buddy={buddy} {...(friend ? { friend } : {})} height={frame} />
      {!watching && (
        <div className="row">
          {p.choices.map((c) => (
            <button
              key={shake?.id === c.id ? `${c.id}:${shake.n}` : c.id}
              type="button"
              className={`option ${cueClass(flow.cue, c.id)} ${picked === c.id ? 'is-right' : ''} ${shake?.id === c.id ? 'shake' : ''}`}
              style={{ width: size + 40, rotate: tiltFor(c.id) }}
              aria-label={c.label}
              onClick={() => tap(c)}
            >
              <Picture asset={c.asset} label="" size={size} />
              <span className="option__label">{c.label}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// src/app/mechanics/Mechanic.tsx
import type { MechanicId } from '../../engine/content/schema';
import { DragToMatch } from './DragToMatch';
import { GiveToBuddy } from './GiveToBuddy';
import { SceneThenChoose } from './SceneThenChoose';
import { Sequence } from './Sequence';
import { SortIntoBins } from './SortIntoBins';
import { TapAll } from './TapAll';
import { TapTheOne } from './TapTheOne';
import type { MechanicProps } from './types';

/** The M1 mechanics (spec §15) by id; scrub and dress-buddy come with Buddy's Home (Plan 1C). */
const MECHANICS: Partial<Record<MechanicId, (props: MechanicProps) => React.JSX.Element>> = {
  'tap-the-one': TapTheOne,
  'tap-all-that-match': TapAll,
  'sort-into-bins': SortIntoBins,
  'drag-to-match': DragToMatch,
  'give-to-buddy': GiveToBuddy,
  'sequence': Sequence,
  'scene-then-choose': SceneThenChoose,
};

export const isPlayable = (mechanic: MechanicId): boolean => mechanic in MECHANICS;

/** The round's mechanic. One that is not built yet throws, and the game's error boundary ends the set gracefully. */
export function Mechanic(props: MechanicProps) {
  const Component = MECHANICS[props.round.mechanic];
  if (!Component) throw new Error(`mechanic ${props.round.mechanic} is not built yet`);
  return <Component {...props} />;
}
```

### Task 21: Screens: the game and its celebration

`Services` gathers what the screens use from outside React — content, storage, the speaker, effects, music, the audio unlock — one per app, fakes in tests. `GameScreen` plays one set (§4.4): the prompt bar with 🔊, progress dots that fill as each round ends, the 🏠 button (back to the Map; the set stays unfinished), Buddy in the corner reacting (hidden where Buddy is on stage: give-to-buddy and the feelings scenes), the round's mechanic over the set's scene. When the set bridges back to a familiar game, Buddy says so before the first prompt (§5.5). Each round's attempt is queued as it ends, and the next round follows after a short pause — a longer one after a kindness scene answered kindly, so its reaction is heard to the end. A finished set records its result and a sticker, then celebrates. A mechanic that throws ends the set gracefully (§13). The celebration (§4.5): confetti and a cheer, the sticker flying to the book, a dance to its 8-second loop (a tap moves on), and — when it is due — "your turn": Buddy shows a jump or a clap, counts to three, cheers whatever happened, and offers "Again?" once. There is no camera, so nothing is judged.

- Create: `src/app/services.ts`, `src/app/screens/Celebration.tsx`, `src/app/screens/celebration.css`, `src/app/screens/GameScreen.tsx`, `src/app/screens/game.css`
- Create: `src/app/testing/services.ts`, `src/app/testing/play.ts`
- Test: `src/app/screens/celebration.test.tsx`, `src/app/screens/game.test.tsx`, `src/app/screens/game-error.test.tsx`

```ts
// src/app/testing/services.ts
// Services for app-level tests: a fresh fake-IndexedDB database, a voice that records, silent sounds.
import type { SpeakRequest } from '../../engine/audio/manager';
import { createDb } from '../../engine/progress/db';
import { Speaker, type Voice } from '../audio/speaker';
import { createStorageHealth, WriteQueue } from '../game/storage';
import { pack } from '../pack';
import type { Services } from '../services';

export class RecordingVoice implements Voice {
  said: SpeakRequest[] = [];
  /** while true, each line goes on "sounding" until release() */
  hold = false;
  private sounding: Array<() => void> = [];
  speak(req: SpeakRequest): Promise<void> {
    this.said.push(req);
    return this.hold ? new Promise<void>((resolve) => this.sounding.push(resolve)) : Promise.resolve();
  }
  release() {
    this.hold = false;
    this.sounding.splice(0).forEach((resolve) => resolve());
  }
  stop() {}
}

let n = 0;

/** Needs fake-indexeddb loaded (import 'fake-indexeddb/auto'). */
export function testServices(): Services & { voice: RecordingVoice; danced: string[] } {
  const voice = new RecordingVoice();
  const storage = createStorageHealth();
  const danced: string[] = [];
  let seed = 1000;
  return {
    voice,
    danced,
    pack,
    db: createDb(`app-test-${++n}`),
    speaker: new Speaker(voice, pack),
    sfx: { unlock() {}, chime() {}, boop() {}, pop() {}, whoosh() {}, sparkle() {} },
    music: { play: (d) => { danced.push(d); }, stop() {} },
    storage,
    writes: new WriteQueue(storage),
    unlockAudio() {},
    speakText() {},
    random: Math.random,
    newSeed: () => ++seed,
    now: () => Date.now(),
  };
}
```

```ts
// src/app/testing/play.ts
// Helpers for screen and app tests: wait for storage and timers, and a child who taps anything.
import { act, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { vi } from 'vitest';
import { createProfile, startSession } from '../../engine/progress/db';
import { monotonicClock } from '../game/clock';
import type { PlayContext } from '../game/setRunner';
import type { Services } from '../services';

/** The fake timers app tests use: IndexedDB's own scheduling (setImmediate) stays real. */
export const useAppTimers = () => vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout', 'setInterval', 'clearInterval'] });

/** Let storage and React catch up: Dexie and fake-indexeddb schedule on timers too, so each pass moves them on by 5 ms. */
export async function settle(times = 20): Promise<void> {
  for (let i = 0; i < times; i++) {
    await act(async () => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(5);
      await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r));
    });
  }
}

export async function wait(ms: number): Promise<void> {
  await act(async () => {
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(ms);
  });
  await settle();
}

/** A child who taps whatever the round offers, until the set is over: Buddy shows whatever is missed. */
export async function playAnyhow(maxSteps = 300): Promise<boolean> {
  const pick = <T,>(xs: readonly T[]) => xs[Math.floor(Math.random() * xs.length)] as T;
  for (let i = 0; i < maxSteps; i++) {
    if (document.querySelector('.celebration')) return true;
    const stage = document.querySelector('.stage');
    if (stage) {
      const drags = [...stage.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-drag-id]')];
      const drops = [...stage.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-drop-id]')].filter((d) => !d.matches('[data-drag-id]'));
      const done = stage.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.done-button');
      const unmatched = [...stage.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button[data-drop-id][aria-pressed=false]')];
      const options = [...stage.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button.option')];
      if (done && Math.random() < 0.4) fireEvent.click(done);
      else if (drags.length > 0) {
        fireEvent.click(pick(drags));
        fireEvent.click(pick(drops));
      } else if (unmatched.length > 1) {
        const a = pick(unmatched);
        fireEvent.click(a);
        fireEvent.click(pick(unmatched.filter((x) => x !== a)));
      } else if (options.length > 0) fireEvent.click(pick(options));
    }
    await wait(1000);
  }
  return false;
}

/** A profile, its first session and the play context a set needs. */
export async function playContextFor(services: Services): Promise<PlayContext> {
  const profile = await createProfile(services.db, { name: 'Asha', buddy: { colour: 'pink', name: 'Gajju', accessory: 'bow' } });
  const session = await startSession(services.db, profile.id);
  return {
    pack: services.pack, db: services.db, profileId: profile.id, sessionIndex: session.index, sessionStartedAt: session.startedAt,
    clock: monotonicClock(0), now: services.now, writes: services.writes, storage: services.storage, random: services.random, newSeed: services.newSeed,
  };
}
```

```tsx
// src/app/screens/celebration.test.tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Celebration, CHEER_MS, DANCE_MS, DEMO_MS, PARTY_MS, STICKER_MS, YOUR_TURN_MS } from './Celebration';

beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

function setup(yourTurn: boolean) {
  const said: string[] = [];
  const music: string[] = [];
  const onDone = vi.fn();
  const services = {
    speaker: { say: (id: string) => { said.push(id); return { caption: id, done: Promise.resolve() }; } },
    sfx: { chime() {}, sparkle() {}, whoosh() {} },
    music: { play: (d: string) => music.push(d), stop: () => music.push('stop') },
    random: () => 0,
  } as never;
  render(<Celebration services={services} look={{ colour: 'blue', accessory: 'cap' }} sticker={{ pictureId: 'objects/cat.svg', shiny: true }} dances={['trunk-twirl', 'elephant-stomp']} yourTurn={yourTurn} onDone={onDone} />);
  const buddyClass = () => screen.getByRole('img', { name: 'Buddy' }).getAttribute('class') ?? '';
  return { said, music, onDone, buddyClass };
}

describe('Celebration', () => {
  it('cheers, flies the sticker to the book, dances its 8-second loop, and is done', () => {
    const t = setup(false);
    expect(t.said).toEqual(['fb.set_done']);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(PARTY_MS + STICKER_MS));
    expect(t.music).toEqual(['trunk-twirl']);
    expect(t.buddyClass()).toContain('buddy--trunk-twirl');
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(DANCE_MS));
    expect(t.onDone).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('a tap during the dance moves on; nothing fires twice after it', () => {
    const t = setup(false);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(PARTY_MS + STICKER_MS + 1000));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Buddy' }));
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000));
    expect(t.onDone).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('your turn: Buddy shows an action, asks the child to copy it, cheers, and offers "Again?" once', () => {
    const t = setup(true);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(PARTY_MS + STICKER_MS + DANCE_MS));
    expect(t.buddyClass()).toContain('buddy--jump');
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(DEMO_MS));
    expect(t.said).toEqual(['fb.set_done', 'fb.your_turn']);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(YOUR_TURN_MS));
    expect(t.said.at(-1)).toBe('fb.your_turn_cheer');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Again'));
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Again')).toBeNull();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(DEMO_MS + YOUR_TURN_MS));
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Again')).toBeNull(); // only once
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(CHEER_MS));
    expect(t.onDone).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('tapping Buddy skips "your turn"', () => {
    const t = setup(true);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(PARTY_MS + STICKER_MS + DANCE_MS + DEMO_MS));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Buddy' }));
    expect(t.onDone).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});
```

```tsx
// src/app/screens/game.test.tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import 'fake-indexeddb/auto';
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { generateSet } from '../../engine/generator/round';
import { EMPTY_HISTORY } from '../../engine/generator/types';
import { recordAttempt } from '../../engine/progress/db';
import { attemptFromRound } from '../../engine/progress/history';
import { WATCH_MS } from '../scenario/vignettes';
import { playAnyhow, playContextFor, settle, useAppTimers, wait } from '../testing/play';
import { testServices } from '../testing/services';
import { DANCE_MS, PARTY_MS, STICKER_MS } from './Celebration';
import { BRIDGE_MAX_MS, GameScreen, NEXT_MS, REACTION_NEXT_MS } from './GameScreen';

beforeEach(() => { useAppTimers(); });
afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

async function setup(topicId: string, before?: (play: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof playContextFor>>) => Promise<void>, holdVoice = false) {
  const services = testServices();
  const play = await playContextFor(services);
  await before?.(play);
  services.voice.hold = holdVoice;
  const onFinished = vi.fn();
  const onLeave = vi.fn();
  const { unmount } = render(<GameScreen services={services} play={play} look={{ colour: 'pink', accessory: 'bow' }} names={{ name: 'Asha', buddy: 'Gajju' }} topicId={topicId} dances={['elephant-stomp']} yourTurn={false} onLeave={onLeave} onFinished={onFinished} />);
  await settle();
  return { services, play, onFinished, onLeave, unmount };
}

describe('GameScreen', () => {
  it('plays a set to the end: each round recorded, the set\'s result, a sticker, the celebration', async () => {
    const t = await setup('good-habits');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Hear it again')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(await playAnyhow()).toBe(true);
    await wait(PARTY_MS + STICKER_MS + DANCE_MS + 500);
    expect(t.onFinished).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    await t.services.writes.idle();
    const sets = await t.services.db.setResults.toArray();
    expect(sets).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await t.services.db.attempts.count()).toBe(sets[0]!.total);
    expect(await t.services.db.stickers.count()).toBe(1);
    expect(t.services.danced).toEqual(['elephant-stomp']);
  }, 30_000);

  it('fills a progress dot as each round ends', async () => {
    await setup('letters');
    const dots = () => document.querySelectorAll('.progress-dots__dot.is-done').length;
    expect(dots()).toBe(0);
    const right = [...document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.stage button.option')];
    for (const b of right) fireEvent.click(b); // one of them is right; the round ends either way within two misses
    await wait(2000);
    expect(dots()).toBe(1);
  });

  it('says the bridge line, captioned, before the first prompt when the set bridges back to a familiar game (spec §5.5)', async () => {
    const t = await setup('big-small', async (play) => {
      const tap = generateSet(play.pack, play.pack.templates.get('big-small.tap')!, { seed: 1, level: 1, history: EMPTY_HISTORY })[0]!;
      const sort = generateSet(play.pack, play.pack.templates.get('big-small.sort')!, { seed: 2, level: 1, history: EMPTY_HISTORY })[0]!;
      const base = { profileId: play.profileId, sessionIndex: play.sessionIndex, hints: 0, durationMs: 1 };
      await recordAttempt(play.db, attemptFromRound(tap, { ...base, at: 1, outcome: 'correct' }));
      await recordAttempt(play.db, attemptFromRound(sort, { ...base, at: 2, outcome: 'shown' }));
    }, true);
    expect(t.services.voice.said.map((r) => r.lineId)).toEqual(['buddy.bridge']); // the prompt waits for it
    expect(screen.getByText("Remember how you did this before? Let's do it that way again!")).toBeTruthy();
    act(() => t.services.voice.release());
    await settle();
    expect(t.services.voice.said[1]?.lineId).toBe('prompt.tap_big');
  });

  it('starts the first round anyway when the bridge line never ends (a stalled voice)', async () => {
    const t = await setup('big-small', async (play) => {
      const tap = generateSet(play.pack, play.pack.templates.get('big-small.tap')!, { seed: 1, level: 1, history: EMPTY_HISTORY })[0]!;
      const sort = generateSet(play.pack, play.pack.templates.get('big-small.sort')!, { seed: 2, level: 1, history: EMPTY_HISTORY })[0]!;
      const base = { profileId: play.profileId, sessionIndex: play.sessionIndex, hints: 0, durationMs: 1 };
      await recordAttempt(play.db, attemptFromRound(tap, { ...base, at: 1, outcome: 'correct' }));
      await recordAttempt(play.db, attemptFromRound(sort, { ...base, at: 2, outcome: 'shown' }));
    }, true);
    expect(t.services.voice.said.map((r) => r.lineId)).toEqual(['buddy.bridge']);
    expect(document.querySelectorAll('.option')).toHaveLength(0); // nothing to tap while Buddy bridges
    await wait(BRIDGE_MAX_MS);
    expect(t.services.voice.said[1]?.lineId).toBe('prompt.tap_big');
    expect(document.querySelectorAll('.option').length).toBeGreaterThan(0); // the options come with the first prompt
  });

  it('forgets its prompt when it goes, so the next set\'s 🔊 has nothing stale to replay', async () => {
    const t = await setup('letters');
    const said = t.services.voice.said.length;
    expect(said).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    t.unmount();
    await t.services.speaker.replayPrompt();
    expect(t.services.voice.said).toHaveLength(said);
  });

  it('after a kindness scene, lets its reaction be heard before the next scene\'s narration', async () => {
    const t = await setup('kind-fair');
    const lines = () => t.services.voice.said.map((r) => r.lineId);
    const scene = [...t.services.pack.scenarios.values()].find((s) => s.narrationLineId === lines()[0]);
    const kind = t.services.pack.objects.get(scene?.choices.find((c) => c.correct)?.objectId ?? '');
    if (!kind) throw new Error(`no kind choice for ${lines()[0]}`);
    await wait(WATCH_MS);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText(kind.name));
    expect(lines().at(-1)).toBe('react.kind');
    await wait(NEXT_MS + 400);
    expect(lines().at(-1)).toBe('react.kind'); // not cut off by the next narration
    await wait(REACTION_NEXT_MS - NEXT_MS);
    expect(lines().at(-1)).toMatch(/^sc\./); // the next scene
  });

  it('the Home button leaves: the rounds played stay recorded, with no result and no sticker', async () => {
    const t = await setup('feelings');
    expect(await t.services.db.setResults.count()).toBe(0);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Home'));
    expect(t.onLeave).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    await t.services.writes.idle();
    expect(await t.services.db.setResults.count()).toBe(0);
    expect(await t.services.db.stickers.count()).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

**File: `src/app/services.ts`**

```ts
// src/app/services.ts
import { createDb, type BuddyDB } from '../engine/progress/db';
import type { ContentPack } from '../engine/content/load';
import { createWebSpeechTts } from '../engine/audio/tts';
import { createMusic, type Music } from './audio/music';
import { createAudioManager, loadManifest, primeHowler, primeVoices, stopWhenHidden, unlockSpeech } from './audio/setup';
import { createSfx, unlockOnEveryTap, type Sfx } from './audio/sfx';
import { Speaker } from './audio/speaker';
import { lazyContext } from './audio/synth';
import { createStorageHealth, WriteQueue, type StorageHealth } from './game/storage';
import { pack } from './pack';

/** Everything the screens use from outside React: content, storage, sound. One per app; fakes in tests. */
export interface Services {
  pack: ContentPack;
  db: BuddyDB;
  speaker: Speaker;
  sfx: Sfx;
  music: Music;
  storage: StorageHealth;
  writes: WriteQueue;
  /** Call synchronously inside the child's first tap, before any await (spec §7: audio starts after a tap). */
  unlockAudio(): void;
  /** Say any text with the device voice: the setup screen's name suggestions, which have no lines. */
  speakText(text: string): void;
  random(): number;
  /** a fresh random 32-bit seed (crypto, which also works outside https) */
  newSeed(): number;
  now(): number;
}

export async function createServices(): Promise<Services> {
  const baseUrl = import.meta.env.BASE_URL;
  const audio = createAudioManager(pack, await loadManifest(baseUrl), baseUrl);
  const context = lazyContext();
  const music = createMusic(context);
  const sfx = createSfx(context);
  unlockOnEveryTap(sfx); // iOS can suspend the context mid-visit (a lock, a call): the next tap resumes it
  const storage = createStorageHealth();
  const tts = createWebSpeechTts();
  primeHowler();
  primeVoices();
  stopWhenHidden(() => {
    audio.stop();
    music.stop();
  });
  return {
    pack,
    db: createDb(),
    speaker: new Speaker(audio, pack),
    sfx,
    music,
    storage,
    writes: new WriteQueue(storage),
    unlockAudio() {
      unlockSpeech();
      sfx.unlock();
    },
    speakText(text) {
      audio.stop();
      void tts.speak(text).catch(() => {});
    },
    random: Math.random,
    newSeed: () => crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0] as number,
    now: Date.now,
  };
}
```

**File: `src/app/screens/Celebration.tsx`**

```tsx
// src/app/screens/Celebration.tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { Sticker } from '../../engine/progress/types';
import { UI_LINES } from '../audio/uiLines';
import { ACTIONS, Buddy, DANCES, type ActionId, type BuddyMove, type DanceId } from '../buddy/Buddy';
import type { BuddyLook } from '../mechanics/types';
import type { Services } from '../services';
import { artUrl } from '../ui/art';
import { useLater } from '../ui/hooks';
import { Picture } from '../ui/Picture';
import './celebration.css';

type Phase = 'party' | 'sticker' | 'dance' | 'demo' | 'your-turn' | 'cheer';

/** Spec §4.5: each dance has an 8-second loop. */
export const DANCE_MS = 8000;
export const PARTY_MS = 1600;
export const STICKER_MS = 1600;
export const DEMO_MS = 1800;
export const YOUR_TURN_MS = 6000;
export const CHEER_MS = 6000;

/**
 * Spec §4.5: after a finished set — confetti and a cheer, the sticker flies to the book, Buddy dances to its 8-second
 * loop (a tap moves on). When `yourTurn`, Buddy then shows an action and asks the child to copy it (1… 2… 3!), cheers
 * whatever happened, and offers "Again?" once; tapping Buddy skips it. There is no camera: nothing is judged.
 */
export function Celebration({ services, look, sticker, dances, yourTurn, onDone }: {
  services: Pick<Services, 'speaker' | 'sfx' | 'music' | 'random'>;
  look: BuddyLook;
  sticker: Pick<Sticker, 'pictureId' | 'shiny'>;
  dances: readonly string[];
  yourTurn: boolean;
  onDone: () => void;
}) {
  const later = useLater();
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>('party');
  const [againUsed, setAgainUsed] = useState(false);
  const step = useRef(0);
  const finished = useRef(false);
  const [{ dance, action }] = useState(() => {
    const known = dances.filter((d): d is DanceId => (DANCES as readonly string[]).includes(d));
    const pool = known.length > 0 ? known : [...DANCES];
    return {
      dance: pool[Math.floor(services.random() * pool.length)] as DanceId,
      action: ACTIONS[Math.floor(services.random() * ACTIONS.length)] as ActionId,
    };
  });

  /** Enter a phase; anything scheduled for an earlier phase is dropped. */
  const go = (next: Phase) => {
    step.current += 1;
    setPhase(next);
  };
  const at = (ms: number, fn: () => void) => {
    const mine = step.current;
    later(() => {
      if (step.current === mine) fn();
    }, ms);
  };
  const finish = () => {
    if (finished.current) return;
    finished.current = true;
    step.current += 1;
    services.music.stop();
    onDone();
  };
  const yourTurnNow = () => {
    go('demo');
    at(DEMO_MS, () => {
      go('your-turn');
      services.speaker.say(UI_LINES.yourTurn);
      at(YOUR_TURN_MS, () => {
        go('cheer');
        services.sfx.chime();
        services.speaker.say(UI_LINES.yourTurnCheer);
        at(CHEER_MS, finish);
      });
    });
  };
  const afterDance = () => {
    services.music.stop();
    if (yourTurn) yourTurnNow();
    else finish();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    services.sfx.sparkle();
    services.speaker.say(UI_LINES.setDone);
    at(PARTY_MS, () => {
      go('sticker');
      services.sfx.whoosh();
      at(STICKER_MS, () => {
        go('dance');
        services.music.play(dance);
        at(DANCE_MS, afterDance);
      });
    });
    return () => services.music.stop();
    // once, on arrival
  }, []);

  const tapBuddy = () => {
    if (phase === 'dance') afterDance();
    else if (phase === 'demo' || phase === 'your-turn' || phase === 'cheer') finish();
  };
  const again = () => {
    setAgainUsed(true);
    services.speaker.say(UI_LINES.yourTurnAgain);
    yourTurnNow();
  };

  const move: BuddyMove = phase === 'dance' ? dance : phase === 'demo' ? action : phase === 'cheer' || phase === 'party' ? 'cheer' : 'idle';
  return (
    <div className="celebration screen" onClick={phase === 'dance' ? afterDance : undefined}>
      {phase === 'party' && (
        <div className="confetti" aria-hidden="true">
          {Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, i) => (
            <i key={i} style={{ left: `${(i * 37) % 100}%`, animationDelay: `${(i % 9) * 0.07}s`, background: ['#ea5a47', '#fcea2b', '#61b2e4', '#b1cc33', '#f4aa41', '#b399c8'][i % 6] }} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className={`celebration__sticker ${phase === 'party' ? '' : 'is-flying'} ${sticker.shiny ? 'is-shiny' : ''}`}>
        <Picture asset={sticker.pictureId} label="your sticker" size={140} />
      </div>
      <button type="button" className="celebration__buddy" aria-label="Buddy" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); tapBuddy(); }}>
        <Buddy {...look} move={move} expression={phase === 'your-turn' ? 'neutral' : 'happy'} size={Math.min(window.innerHeight * 0.62, 460)} />
      </button>
      {phase === 'your-turn' && <div className="celebration__count" aria-hidden="true"><b>1</b><b>2</b><b>3</b></div>}
      {phase === 'cheer' && !againUsed && (
        <button type="button" className="icon-button celebration__again" aria-label="Again" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); again(); }}>
          <img src={artUrl('ui/again.svg')} alt="" />
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css
/* src/app/screens/celebration.css */
.celebration { z-index: 50; align-items: center; justify-content: center; background: rgba(246, 239, 226, 0.94) var(--grain); }
.celebration__buddy { display: grid; place-items: center; cursor: pointer; background: none; border: 0; padding: 0; }
.celebration__sticker { position: absolute; left: 50%; top: 18%; transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1.3); padding: 14px; background: var(--card); border-radius: 26px; box-shadow: var(--shadow); transition: transform 1.2s cubic-bezier(.5, -0.3, .3, 1), left 1.2s cubic-bezier(.5, -0.3, .3, 1), top 1.2s cubic-bezier(.5, -0.3, .3, 1), opacity 1.2s; z-index: 2; }
.celebration__sticker.is-flying { left: calc(100% - 60px); top: 50px; transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0.35); opacity: 0.2; }
.celebration__sticker.is-shiny { box-shadow: 0 0 0 6px #ffd23f, 0 0 30px 10px rgba(255, 210, 63, 0.7), var(--shadow); }
.celebration__count { position: absolute; bottom: 8%; display: flex; gap: 60px; font-size: 90px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--ink); }
.celebration__count b { opacity: 0; animation: count-in 0.5s ease-out forwards; }
.celebration__count b:nth-child(1) { animation-delay: 2.6s; }
.celebration__count b:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: 3.4s; }
.celebration__count b:nth-child(3) { animation-delay: 4.2s; }
@keyframes count-in { from { opacity: 0; transform: scale(0.3); } to { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1); } }
.celebration__again { position: absolute; right: 8%; bottom: 10%; transform: scale(1.4); }
.confetti { position: absolute; inset: 0; pointer-events: none; overflow: hidden; }
.confetti i { position: absolute; top: -20px; width: 14px; height: 22px; border-radius: 3px; animation: confetti-fall 1.8s ease-in forwards; }
@keyframes confetti-fall { to { transform: translateY(110vh) rotate(540deg); } }
```

**File: `src/app/screens/GameScreen.tsx`**

```tsx
// src/app/screens/GameScreen.tsx
import { Component, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { addSticker } from '../../engine/progress/db';
import type { Sticker } from '../../engine/progress/types';
import type { Names } from '../audio/speaker';
import { UI_LINES } from '../audio/uiLines';
import { Buddy, type BuddyExpression, type BuddyMove } from '../buddy/Buddy';
import { finishSet, prepareSet, recordRound, type PlayContext, type PlaySet, type RoundResult } from '../game/setRunner';
import { newSticker } from '../game/stickers';
import type { FlowEnd, Reaction } from '../game/useRoundFlow';
import { Mechanic } from '../mechanics/Mechanic';
import type { BuddyLook } from '../mechanics/types';
import { VIGNETTES } from '../scenario/vignettes';
import type { Services } from '../services';
import { artUrl } from '../ui/art';
import { useLater } from '../ui/hooks';
import { IconButton } from '../ui/IconButton';
import { ProgressDots } from '../ui/ProgressDots';
import { PromptBar } from '../ui/PromptBar';
import { Celebration } from './Celebration';
import './game.css';

/** Spec §13: an exception inside a mechanic ends the set gracefully and goes back to the Map (stickers kept). */
class MechanicBoundary extends Component<{ onError: (error: unknown) => void; children: ReactNode }, { failed: boolean }> {
  override state = { failed: false };
  static getDerivedStateFromError() {
    return { failed: true };
  }
  override componentDidCatch(error: unknown) {
    this.props.onError(error);
  }
  override render() {
    return this.state.failed ? null : this.props.children;
  }
}

export interface GameProps {
  services: Services;
  play: PlayContext;
  look: BuddyLook;
  names: Names;
  topicId: string;
  /** the dances Buddy knows (Unlocks.dances) */
  dances: readonly string[];
  /** whether this set ends with "your turn" (spec §4.5: every 2nd–3rd set) */
  yourTurn: boolean;
  /** the Home button, or a failure: back to the Map; the set stays unfinished */
  onLeave: () => void;
  /** the set and its celebration are over */
  onFinished: () => void;
  quiet?: { first: number; every: number };
}

/** Pause after a round's feedback before the next round. */
export const NEXT_MS = 1600;
/**
 * The pause after a kindness scene answered kindly: its reaction ("That was kind! Look, your friend is smiling now",
 * about 4.2 s at the voice's 0.9 rate) is heard to the end while the friend smiles; the next narration would cut it.
 */
export const REACTION_NEXT_MS = 4500;
/** The longest the first prompt waits for the bridge line: a stalled clip or speech engine may never settle. */
export const BRIDGE_MAX_MS = 8000;

/**
 * One set (spec §4.4): the prompt bar with 🔊, progress dots, the Home button, Buddy in the corner, the round's
 * mechanic on the set's scene. Each round's attempt is recorded as it ends; a finished set records its result and
 * earns a sticker, then celebrates.
 */
export function GameScreen({ services, play, look, names, topicId, dances, yourTurn, onLeave, onFinished, quiet }: GameProps) {
  const later = useLater();
  const [set, setSet] = useState<PlaySet | null>(null);
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  // the first round's mechanic waits for its prompt: while the bridge line plays it is neither tappable nor timing a quiet spell
  const [begun, setBegun] = useState(false);
  const [caption, setCaption] = useState('');
  const [roundsDone, setRoundsDone] = useState(0);
  const [sticker, setSticker] = useState<Omit<Sticker, 'id'> | null>(null);
  // `beat` counts reactions: it keys Buddy, so the same move plays again, and only the latest one goes back to idle
  const [buddy, setBuddy] = useState<{ move: BuddyMove; expression: BuddyExpression; beat: number }>({ move: 'idle', expression: 'neutral', beat: 0 });
  const beat = useRef(0);
  const results = useRef<RoundResult[]>([]);
  const startedAt = useRef(0);

  const begin = (s: PlaySet, i: number) => {
    const round = s.rounds[i];
    if (!round) return;
    setIndex(i);
    setBegun(true);
    setCaption(services.speaker.prompt(round).caption);
    startedAt.current = services.now();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    prepareSet(play, topicId).then(
      async (s) => {
        if (!live) return;
        setSet(s);
        if (s.bridgeFrom) {
          // spec §5.5: Buddy links the forms aloud first, its words in the caption meanwhile
          const said = services.speaker.say(UI_LINES.bridge);
          setCaption(said.caption);
          await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
            void said.done.then(resolve);
            later(resolve, BRIDGE_MAX_MS);
          });
        }
        if (live) begin(s, 0);
      },
      (e: unknown) => {
        console.error('could not start the set', e);
        if (live) onLeave();
      },
    );
    return () => {
      live = false;
      services.speaker.stop();
      services.speaker.clearPrompt(); // the next set's 🔊 must not replay this one's prompt
    };
    // a GameScreen plays one set: it starts once
  }, []);

  const leave = () => {
    services.speaker.stop();
    services.music.stop();
    onLeave();
  };

  const react = (r: Reaction) => {
    const mine = ++beat.current;
    setBuddy({ ...(r === 'cheer' ? { move: 'cheer', expression: 'happy' } : r === 'hmm' ? { move: 'hmm', expression: 'thinking' } : { move: 'point', expression: 'neutral' }), beat: mine });
    later(() => {
      if (beat.current === mine) setBuddy({ move: 'idle', expression: 'neutral', beat: mine });
    }, 1500);
  };

  const onEnd = (end: FlowEnd) => {
    if (!set) return;
    const round = set.rounds[index];
    if (!round) return;
    const result: RoundResult = { ...end, durationMs: services.now() - startedAt.current };
    recordRound(play, round, result);
    results.current = [...results.current, result];
    setRoundsDone(results.current.length);
    const p = round.payload;
    // a kind answer is said with its reaction; one Buddy showed is not
    const reaction = p.kind === 'scenario' && p.choices.some((c) => c.correct && c.reactionLineId);
    later(() => {
      if (index + 1 < set.rounds.length) {
        begin(set, index + 1);
        return;
      }
      finishSet(play, set, results.current);
      const earned = newSticker(play.profileId, play.clock.now(), set.template.id, set.rounds, services.random);
      play.writes.add(() => addSticker(play.db, earned));
      services.speaker.clearPrompt();
      setSticker(earned);
    }, end.outcome === 'shown' ? NEXT_MS - 400 : reaction ? REACTION_NEXT_MS : NEXT_MS);
  };

  if (sticker) return <Celebration services={services} look={look} sticker={sticker} dances={dances} yourTurn={yourTurn} onDone={onFinished} />;

  const round = set?.rounds[index];
  const scene = round ? services.pack.scenes.get(round.sceneId) : undefined;
  const payload = round?.payload;
  const buddyOnStage = round?.mechanic === 'give-to-buddy' || (payload?.kind === 'scenario' && VIGNETTES[payload.vignette]?.actors.some((a) => a.who === 'buddy'));
  return (
    <div className="screen game">
      {scene && <img className="game__scene" src={artUrl(scene.asset)} alt="" />}
      <header className="game__bar">
        <IconButton icon="ui/home.svg" label="Home" onClick={leave} />
        <PromptBar caption={caption} onReplay={() => void services.speaker.replayPrompt()} />
        {set && <ProgressDots total={set.rounds.length} done={roundsDone} />}
      </header>
      {round && begun && (
        <MechanicBoundary key={`${round.seed}:${round.index}`} onError={(e) => { console.error('a mechanic failed', e); leave(); }}>
          <Mechanic
            round={round}
            deps={{ speaker: services.speaker, sfx: services.sfx }}
            names={names}
            look={look}
            onEnd={onEnd}
            onReact={react}
            {...(quiet ? { quiet } : {})}
          />
        </MechanicBoundary>
      )}
      {!buddyOnStage && <div className="game__buddy"><Buddy key={buddy.beat} {...look} move={buddy.move} expression={buddy.expression} size={150} /></div>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css
.game__bar { position: relative; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px; padding: 12px 18px 2px; padding-top: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-top)); }
```

### Task 22: Screens: the Map, Hello, setting up Buddy, the sticker book

The Map (§4.3): five islands on one sideways-scrolling sea, every spot open, each a signpost picture (Task 4). Buddy points at a suggested spot and says so — never the island or topic just played (§5.4 rule 6), from a history that includes the last set. Hello (§4.1): one child, one big Buddy to tap — the tap that unlocks audio (§7); several, a Buddy each, no reading needed. Setup is for a grown-up (§4.7): the child's name (needed: lines greet the child by it), Buddy's colour, a name typed or chosen from suggestions spoken aloud, an accessory; it recommends adding Buddy to the home screen (§10) and credits OpenMoji. The sticker book (§4.6): a page per island, opening on the latest sticker's page, with empty places shown so a page is never a bare "nothing here".

**Files:**
- Create: `src/app/screens/islands.ts`, `src/app/screens/MapScreen.tsx`, `src/app/screens/map.css`, `src/app/screens/Hello.tsx`, `src/app/screens/hello.css`, `src/app/screens/Setup.tsx`, `src/app/screens/setup.css`, `src/app/screens/StickerBook.tsx`, `src/app/screens/stickers.css`
- Test: `src/app/screens/screens.test.tsx`, `src/app/screens/map.test.tsx`

```tsx
// src/app/screens/screens.test.tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { Profile, Sticker } from '../../engine/progress/types';
import { pack } from '../pack';
import { Hello, lookOf } from './Hello';
import { islandSpots } from './islands';
import { Setup } from './Setup';
import { StickerBook } from './StickerBook';

const profile = (id: string, name: string, colour = 'pink', accessory = 'bow'): Profile => ({ id, name, level: 'lkg', buddy: { colour, name: 'Gajju', accessory }, createdAt: 1 });

describe('Setup', () => {
  it('needs the child\'s name, offers spoken names for Buddy, and hands over the choices', () => {
    const onDone = vi.fn();
    const spoken: string[] = [];
    render(<Setup onDone={onDone} speakText={(t) => spoken.push(t)} />);
    const go = screen.getByText("Let's play!") as HTMLButtonElement;
    expect(go.disabled).toBe(true);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Child's name"), { target: { value: '  Asha ' } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('green'));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('Appu'));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('glasses'));
    expect(spoken).toEqual(['Appu']);
    fireEvent.click(go);
    expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ name: 'Asha', buddy: { colour: 'green', name: 'Appu', accessory: 'glasses' } });
    expect(screen.getByText(/add Buddy to the home screen/)).toBeTruthy(); // spec §10: recommended on the first run
  });
});

describe('Hello', () => {
  it('one child: one big Buddy to tap; several: one each; the + is for a grown-up', () => {
    const onStart = vi.fn();
    const onAdd = vi.fn();
    const { unmount } = render(<Hello profiles={[profile('a', 'Asha')]} greeting={null} onStart={onStart} onAddChild={onAdd} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText("Asha's Gajju"));
    expect(onStart).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ id: 'a' }));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Add a child (grown-ups)'));
    expect(onAdd).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    unmount();
    render(<Hello profiles={[profile('a', 'Asha'), profile('b', 'Ravi', 'blue', 'cap')]} greeting={null} onStart={onStart} onAddChild={onAdd} />);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: /Gajju/ })).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('uses a safe look for a profile saved with an unknown colour or accessory', () => {
    expect(lookOf(profile('x', 'X', 'teal', 'crown'))).toEqual({ colour: 'grey', accessory: 'cap' });
  });
});

describe('the Map\'s islands', () => {
  it('has five islands with two playable spots each in M1', () => {
    expect(islandSpots(pack).map((i) => `${i.subject}:${i.topics.length}`)).toEqual(['literacy:2', 'phonics:2', 'numeracy:2', 'awareness:2', 'wellness:2']);
  });
});

describe('StickerBook', () => {
  it('opens on the page of the latest sticker, shows each island\'s own, and empty places for more', () => {
    const stickers: Sticker[] = [
      { id: 1, profileId: 'a', at: 1, pictureId: 'objects/cat.svg', source: 'rhyming.match', shiny: false },
      { id: 2, profileId: 'a', at: 2, pictureId: 'feelings/happy.svg', source: 'feelings.tap', shiny: true },

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Feelings Forest').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('.book__sticker.is-shiny')).toHaveLength(1);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Sound Garden'));
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('.book__sticker:not(.is-empty)')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('.book__sticker.is-empty')).toHaveLength(11);
  });
});
```

```ts
// src/app/screens/islands.ts
import type { ContentPack } from '../../engine/content/load';
import { SUBJECTS, type SubjectId, type Topic } from '../../engine/content/schema';
import { isPlayable } from '../mechanics/Mechanic';

/** Spec §4.3: the five islands of the Play Map, one per subject. */
export const ISLANDS: Readonly<Record<SubjectId, { title: string; icon: string; colour: string }>> = {
  literacy: { title: 'Letter Land', icon: 'islands/literacy.svg', colour: 'var(--literacy)' },
  phonics: { title: 'Sound Garden', icon: 'islands/phonics.svg', colour: 'var(--phonics)' },
  numeracy: { title: 'Number Hill', icon: 'islands/numeracy.svg', colour: 'var(--numeracy)' },
  awareness: { title: 'Our World', icon: 'islands/awareness.svg', colour: 'var(--awareness)' },
  wellness: { title: 'Feelings Forest', icon: 'islands/wellness.svg', colour: 'var(--wellness)' },
};

/** Each island's topic spots: topics with at least one game the app can play, in pack order. */
export function islandSpots(pack: ContentPack): Array<{ subject: SubjectId; topics: Topic[] }> {
  return SUBJECTS.map((subject) => ({
    subject,
    topics: [...pack.topics.values()].filter((t) => t.subject === subject && (pack.templatesByTopic.get(t.id) ?? []).some((tpl) => isPlayable(tpl.mechanic))),
  }));
}
```
